      local name of the SOAPHeader that needs to be present in the message.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RequiredParts/sp:Header/@Namespace</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required attribute indicates the
      namespace of the SOAP header that needs to be present in the message.</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc161826521" name="_Toc161826521"></a><a id="_Toc104103938"
        name="_Toc104103938"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>5<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
        <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Token</span></a> <span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Assertions</span></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Token assertions
      specify the type of tokens to use to protect or bind tokens and claims to the
      message. These assertions do not recommend usage of a Policy Subject. Assertions
      which contain them SHOULD recommend a policy attachment point. With the exception
      of transport token assertions, the token assertions defined in this section are not
      specific to any particular security binding.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc96244141" name="_Toc96244141"></a><a id="_Toc95877087"
      name="_Toc95877087"></a><a id="_Toc95113101" name="_Toc95113101"></a><a
      id="_Toc92518193" name="_Toc92518193"></a><a id="_Toc161826522"
      name="_Toc161826522"></a><a id="_Toc104103939" name="_Toc104103939"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.1</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Token Inclusion</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any token assertion
      may also carry an optional</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute. The schema type of this
      attribute is</span> <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>xs:anyURI</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>. This attribute indicates whether the
      token should be included, that is written, in the message or whether cryptographic
      operations utilize an external reference mechanism to refer to the key represented
      by the token. This attribute is defined as a global attribute in the
      WS-SecurityPolicy namespace and is intended to be used by any specification that
      defines token assertions.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826523" name="_Toc161826523"></a><a id="_Toc104103940"
      name="_Toc104103940"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>5.1.1</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Token Inclusion
      Values</span></a></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following table
      describes the set of valid token inclusion mechanisms supported by this
      specification:</span></p>
      <table class="MsoNormalTable" border="1" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0"
      width="636" style='width:477.05pt;border-collapse:collapse;border:none'>
        <tr>
          <td width="319" valign="top"
          style='width:239.4pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>http://docs.oasis-open.org/ws-sx/ws-securitypolicy/200702/IncludeToken/Never</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="317" valign="top"
          style='width:237.65pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-left:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The token MUST
            NOT be included in any messages sent between the initiator and the recipient;
            rather, an external reference to the token should be used.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="319" valign="top"
          style='width:239.4pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>http://docs.oasis-open.org/ws-sx/ws-securitypolicy/200702/IncludeToken/Once</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="317" valign="top"
          style='width:237.65pt;border-top:none;border-left: none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The token MUST
            be included in only one message sent from the initiator to the recipient.
            References to the token MAY use an internal reference mechanism. Subsequent
            related messages sent between the recipient and the initiator may refer to
            the token using an external reference mechanism.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="319" valign="top"
          style='width:239.4pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>http://docs.oasis-open.org/ws-sx/ws-securitypolicy/200702/IncludeToken/AlwaysToRecipient</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="317" valign="top"
          style='width:237.65pt;border-top:none;border-left: none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The token MUST
            be included in all messages sent from initiator to the recipient. The token
            MUST NOT be included in messages sent from the recipient to the
            initiator.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="319" valign="top"
          style='width:239.4pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>http://docs.oasis-open.org/ws-sx/ws-securitypolicy/200702/IncludeToken/AlwaysToInitiator</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="317" valign="top"
          style='width:237.65pt;border-top:none;border-left: none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The token MUST
            be included in all messages sent from the recipient to the initiator. The
            token MUST NOT be included in messages sent from the initiator to the
            recipient.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="319" valign="top"
          style='width:239.4pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>http://docs.oasis-open.org/ws-sx/ws-securitypolicy/200702/IncludeToken/Always</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="317" valign="top"
          style='width:237.65pt;border-top:none;border-left: none;border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The token MUST
            be included in all messages sent between the initiator and the recipient.
            This is the default behavior.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
      </table>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: In examples,
      the namespace URI is replaced with "..." for brevity. For example,
      .../IncludeToken/Never is actually
      http://docs.oasis-open.org/ws-sx/ws-securitypolicy/200702/IncludeToken/Never.</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Other token inclusion URI values MAY
      be defined but are out-of-scope of this specification.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The default behavior
      characteristics defined by this specification if this attribute is not specified on
      a token assertion are .../IncludeToken/Always.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826524" name="_Toc161826524"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.1.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Token Inclusion and Token
      References</span></a></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A token assertion
      may carry a sp:IncludeToken attribute that requires that the token be included in
      the message. The Web Services Security specifications [</span><a
      href="#wss10"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS10</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>,</span> <a href="#wss11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS11</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>] define mechanisms for how tokens are
      included in a message.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Several Token
      assertions (see Section 5.3) support mechanisms for referencing tokens in addition
      to Direct References, for example external URI references or references using a
      Thumbprint.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Certain combination
      of sp:IncludeToken value and token reference assertions can result in a token
      appearing in a message more than once. For example, if a token assertion carries a
      sp:IncludeToken attribute with a value of '.../Always' and that token assertion
      also contains a nested sp:RequireEmbeddedTokenReference (see Section 5.3.3)
      assertion, then the token would be included twice in the message. While such
      combinations are not in error, they are probably best avoided for efficiency
      reasons.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a token assertion
      contains multiple reference assertions, then references to that token are required
      to contain all the specified reference types. For example, if a token assertion
      contains nested sp:RequireIssuerSerialReference and sp:RequireThumbprintReference
      assertions then references to that token contain both reference forms. Again, while
      such combinations are not in error, they are probably best avoided for efficiency
      reasons.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826525" name="_Toc161826525">5.2 Token Issuer and Required
      Claims</a></h2>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826526" name="_Toc161826526">5.2.1 Token Issuer</a></h3>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>Any token assertion may
      also carry an optional sp:Issuer element. The schema type of this element is
      wsa:EndpointReferenceType. This element indicates the token issuing authority by
      pointing to the issuer endpoint address. This element is defined as a global
      element in the WS-SecurityPolicy namespace and is intended to be used by any
      specification that defines token assertions.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826527" name="_Toc161826527">5.2.2 Token Issuer Name</a></h3>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>Any token assertion may
      also carry an optional sp:IssuerName element. The schema type of this element is
      xs:anyURI. This element indicated the token issuing authority by pointing to the
      issuer by using its logical name. This element is defined as a global element in
      the WS-SecurityPolicy namespace and is intended to be used by any specification
      that defines token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>It is out of scope of
      this specification how the relationship between the issuer&rsquo;s logical name and
      the physical manifestation of the issuer in the security token is
      defined.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>While both sp:Issuer and
      sp:IssuerName elements are optional they are also mutually exclusive and cannot be
      specified both at the same time.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826528" name="_Toc161826528">5.2.3 Required Claims</a></h3>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>Any token assertion may
      also carry an optional wst:Claims element. The element content is defined in the
      WS-Trust namespace. This specification does not further define or limit the content
      of this element or the wst:Claims/@Dialect attribute as it is out of scope of this
      document.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>This element indicates
      the required claims that the security token must contain in order to satisfy the
      requirements of the token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>Individual token
      assertions may further limit what claims may be specified for that specific token
      assertion.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826529" name="_Toc161826529">5.2.4 Processing Rules and Token
      Matching</a></h3>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>The sender is free to
      compose the requirements expressed by token assertions inside the receiver&rsquo;s
      policy to as many tokens as it sees fit. As long as the union of all tokens in the
      received message contains the required set of claims from required token issuers
      the message is valid according to the receiver&rsquo;s policy.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";color:black'>For example if the
      receiver&rsquo;s policy contains two token assertions, one requires IssuedToken
      from issuer A with claims C1 and C2 and the second requires IssuedToken from issuer
      B with claims C3 and C4, the sender can satisfy such requirements with any of the
      following security token decomposition:</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
      <span style='color:black'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <ol style='margin-top:0in' start="1" type="1">
        <li class="MsoNormal"
        style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in; margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
        Two tokens, T1 and T2. T1 is issued by issuer A and contains claims C1 and C2 and
        T2 is issued by issuer B and contains claims C3 and C4.</li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"
        style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in; margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
        Three tokens, T1, T2 and T3. T1 is issued by issuer A and contains claim C1, T2
        is also issued by issuer A and contains claim C2 and T3 is issued by issuer B and
        contains claims C3 and C4.</li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"
        style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in; margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
        Three tokens, T1, T2 and T3. T1 is issued by issuer A and contains claims C1 and
        C2, T2 is issued by issuer B and contains claim C3 and T3 is also issued by
        issuer B and contains claim C4.</li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"
        style='color:black;margin-top:0in;margin-bottom:0in; margin-bottom:.0001pt;text-autospace:none'>
        Four tokens, T1, T2, T3 and T4. T1 is issued by issuer A and contains claim C1,
        T2 is also issued by issuer A and contains claim C2, T3 is issued by issuer B and
        contains claim C3 and T4 is also issued by issuer B and contains claim C4.</li>
      </ol>
      <h2><a id="_Toc99269242" name="_Toc99269242"></a><a id="_Toc161826530"
      name="_Toc161826530"></a><a id="_Toc104103941" name="_Toc104103941"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.3</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Token Properties</span></a></h2>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826531" name="_Toc161826531"></a><a id="_Toc104103942"
      name="_Toc104103942"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>5.3.1</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Derived Keys]
      Property</span></a></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether derived keys should be used as defined in
      WS-SecureConversation. If the value is 'true', derived keys MUST be used. If the
      value is 'false', derived keys MUST NOT be used. The value of this property applies
      to a specific token. The value of this property is populated by assertions specific
      to the token. The default value for this property is 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>See the [Explicit
      Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Key] properties below for information on how
      particular forms of derived keys are specified.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Where the key
      material associated with a token is asymmetric, this property applies to the use of
      symmetric keys encrypted with the key material associated with the
      token.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826532" name="_Toc161826532"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.3.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Explicit Derived Keys]
      Property</span></a></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether Explicit Derived Keys (see Section 7 of [</span><a
      href="#wssecureconversation"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-SecureConversation</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>]) are allowed. If the value is 'true'
      then Explicit Derived Keys MAY be used. If the value is 'false' then Explicit
      Derived Keys MUST NOT be used.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826533" name="_Toc161826533"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.3.3</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Implied Derived Keys]
      Property</span></a></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether Implied Derived Keys (see Section 7.3 of
      [WS-SecureConversation]) are allowed. If the value is 'true' then Implied Derived
      Keys MAY be used. If the value is 'false' then Implied Derived Keys MUST NOT be
      used.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826534" name="_Toc161826534"></a><a id="_Toc104103943"
      name="_Toc104103943"></a><a id="_Toc133985015" name="_Toc133985015"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Token Assertion</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Types</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      sections describe the token assertions defined as part of this
      specification.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826535" name="_Toc161826535"></a><a id="_Toc104103944"
      name="_Toc104103944"></a><a id="_Toc99269243" name="_Toc99269243"></a><a
      id="_UsernameToken_Assertion" name="_UsernameToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.1</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>UsernameToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement to include a username token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There are cases
      where encrypting the UsernameToken is reasonable. For example:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When transport security is not
      used.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When a plaintext password is
      used.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When a weak password hash is
      used.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When the username needs to be protected,
      e.g. for privacy reasons.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When the
      UsernameToken is to be encrypted it SHOULD be listed as a
      SignedEncryptedSupportingToken (Section 8.5), EndorsingEncryptedSupportingToken
      (Section 8.6) or SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingToken (Section 8.7).</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:UsernameToken sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..." ...
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |
        &nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:NoPassword ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:HashPassword ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp; &nbsp;(<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;(<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssUsernameToken10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssUsernameToken11 ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:UsernameToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>UsernameToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>UsernameToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains reference to the issuer of the
      sp:UsernameToken.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:UsernameToken
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:UsernameToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:NoPassword</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that indicates that the wsse:Password element MUST NOT be present in the
      Username token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:HashPassword</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that indicates that the wsse:Password element MUST be present in the
      Username token and that the content of the wsse:Password element MUST contain a
      hash of the timestamp, nonce and password as defined in [WSS: Username Token
      Profile].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived Keys] properties for
      this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to
      'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for
      this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to
      'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssUsernameToken10</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that indicates that a Username token should be used as defined in
      [</span><a href="#wssusername10"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:UsernameTokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:UsernameToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssUsernameToken11</span></p>
      <p class="Definitionterm"
      style='margin-top:4.0pt;margin-right:0in;margin-bottom: 4.0pt;margin-left:.5in'>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; font-weight:normal'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a Username token should be used
      as defined in [</span><a href="#wssusername11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";font-weight: normal'>WSS:UsernameTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; font-weight:normal'>].</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826536" name="_Toc161826536"></a><a id="_Toc104103945"
      name="_Toc104103945"></a><a id="_Toc99269244" name="_Toc99269244"></a><a
      id="_Toc96244142" name="_Toc96244142"></a><a id="_Toc95877088"
      name="_Toc95877088"></a><a id="_Toc95113102" name="_Toc95113102"></a><a
      id="_Toc92518194" name="_Toc92518194"></a><a id="_IssuedToken_Assertion"
      name="_IssuedToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>IssuedToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for an issued token, which is one issued by some token
      issuer using the mechanisms defined in WS-Trust. This assertion is used in
      3<sup>rd</sup> party scenarios. For example, the initiator may need to request a
      SAML token from a given token issuer in order to secure messages sent to the
      recipient.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:IssuedToken sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..." ...
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<i>wsa:EndpointReferenceType</i>&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate TrustVersion="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"?
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExternalReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireInternalReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:IssuedToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>an</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>IssuedToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains a reference to the issuer for
      the issued token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:IssuedToken
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element contains elements which MUST be copied into the wst:SecondaryParameters of
      the RST request sent to the specified issuer. Note: the initiator is not required
      to understand the contents of this element.</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>See Appendix B
      for details of the content of this element.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate/@TrustVersion</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional attribute contains a
      WS-Trust specification namespace URI identifying the version of WS-Trust referenced
      by the contents of this element.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:IssuedToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys] and [Implied
      Derived Keys]&nbsp; &nbsp;properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived Keys] properties for
      this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to
      'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for
      this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property for this token to
      'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireInternalReference</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that indicates whether an internal reference is required when referencing
      this token.<br />
      Note: This reference will be supplied by the issuer of the token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IssuedToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExternalReference</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional element is a policy
      assertion that indicates whether an external reference is required when referencing
      this token.<br />
      Note: This reference will be supplied by the issuer of the token.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: The
      IssuedToken may or may not be associated with key material and such key material
      may be symmetric or asymmetric. The Binding assertion will imply the type of key
      associated with this token. Services may also include information in the</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element to explicitly define the
      expected key type. See</span> <a href="#AppendixB"><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Appendix B</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>for details of the</span> <span
      class="CodeChar"><span
      style='font-size:9.0pt'>sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826537" name="_Toc161826537"></a><a id="_Toc104103946"
      name="_Toc104103946"></a><a id="_Toc99269245" name="_Toc99269245"></a><a
      id="_Toc96244143" name="_Toc96244143"></a><a id="_Toc95877089"
      name="_Toc95877089"></a><a id="_Toc95113103" name="_Toc95113103"></a><a
      id="_Toc92518195" name="_Toc92518195"></a><a id="_X509Token_Assertion"
      name="_X509Token_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.3</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>X509Token Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a binary security token carrying an X509
      token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:X509Token sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..." ...
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |
        &nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;
        |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireIssuerSerialReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireEmbeddedTokenReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireThumbprintReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509V3Token10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509V1Token11 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509V3Token11 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token11 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token11 ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>X509Token</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>an</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>X509Token</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains reference to the issuer of the
      sp:X509Token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:X509Token
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:X509Token
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a key identifier reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireIssuerSerialReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an issuer serial reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireEmbeddedTokenReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an embedded token reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireThumbprintReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a thumbprint reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509V3Token10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an X509 Version 3 token should be
      used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssx50910"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:X509TokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an X509 PKCS7 token should be
      used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssx50910"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:X509TokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an X509 PKI Path Version 1 token
      should be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssx50910"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:X509TokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509V1Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an X509 Version 1 token should be
      used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssx50911"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509V3Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an X509 Version 3 token should be
      used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssx50911"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509Pkcs7Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an X509 PKCS7 token should be
      used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssx50911"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:X509Token/wsp:Policy/sp:WssX509PkiPathV1Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an X509 PKI Path Version 1 token
      should be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssx50911"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:X509TokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826538" name="_Toc161826538"></a><a id="_Toc104103947"
      name="_Toc104103947"></a><a id="_Toc99269248" name="_Toc99269248"></a><a
      id="_Toc96244146" name="_Toc96244146"></a><a id="_Toc95877092"
      name="_Toc95877092"></a><a id="_Toc95113106" name="_Toc95113106"></a><a
      id="_Toc92518198" name="_Toc92518198"></a><a id="_KerberosToken_Assertion"
      name="_KerberosToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.4</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>KerberosToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a Kerberos token [</span><a href="#wsskerb11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:KerberosToken1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>]</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:<span class="CodeChar">KerberosToken</span>
        sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |
        &nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssKerberosV5ApReqToken11 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssGssKerberosV5ApReqToken11 ...
        /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        <br />
        </p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:KerberosToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>KerberosToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>KerberosV5ApReqToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains reference to the issuer of the
      sp:KerberosToken.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:KerberosToken
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:KerberosToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a key identifier reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="Definition" style='margin-left:0in'><a id="_Toc96244147"
      name="_Toc96244147"></a><a id="_Toc95877093" name="_Toc95877093"></a><a
      id="_Toc95113107" name="_Toc95113107"></a><a id="_Toc92518199"
      name="_Toc92518199"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssKerberosV5ApReqToken11</span></a></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a Kerberos Version 5 AP-REQ token
      should be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wsskerb11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:KerberosTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KerberosToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssGssKerberosV5ApReqToken11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a GSS Kerberos Version 5 AP-REQ
      token should be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wsskerb11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:KerberosTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826539" name="_Toc161826539"></a><a id="_Toc104103948"
      name="_Toc104103948"></a><a id="_SpnegoContextToken_Assertion"
      name="_SpnegoContextToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.5</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SpnegoContextToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a SecurityContextToken obtained by executing an n-leg
      RST/RSTR SPNEGO binary negotiation protocol with the Web Service, as defined in
      WS-Trust.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SpnegoContextToken sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..." ...
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<i>wsa:EndpointReferenceType</i>&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustNotSendCancel ... /&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustNotSendAmend ... /&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustNotSendRenew ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SpnegoContextToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SpnegoContextToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains a reference to the issuer for
      the Spnego Context Token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><a id="_Toc96244148" name="_Toc96244148"></a><a
      id="_Toc95877094" name="_Toc95877094"></a><a id="_Toc95113108"
      name="_Toc95113108"></a><a id="_Toc92518200" name="_Toc92518200"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/sp:IssuerName</span></a></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:SpnegoContextToken
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:SpnegoContextToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendCancel</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the STS issuing the SP/Nego token
      does not support SCT/Cancel RST messages. If this assertion is missing it means
      that SCT/Cancel RST messages are supported by the STS.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendAmend</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the STS issuing the SP/Nego token
      does not support SCT/Amend RST messages. If this assertion is missing it means that
      SCT/Amend RST messages are supported by the STS.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendRenew</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the STS issuing the SP/Nego token
      does not support SCT/Renew RST messages. If this assertion is missing it means that
      SCT/Renew RST messages are supported by the STS.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826540" name="_Toc161826540"></a><a id="_Toc104103949"
      name="_Toc104103949"></a><a id="_SecurityContextToken_Assertion"
      name="_SecurityContextToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.6</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SecurityContextToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a SecurityContextToken token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SecurityContextToken sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..."
        ... &gt;<br />
        (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |
        &nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExternalUriReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SC13SecurityContextToken... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SecurityContextToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SecurityContextToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a SecurityContextToken assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains reference to the issuer of the
      sp:SecurityContextToken.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the
      sp:SecurityContextToken issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:SecurityContextToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExternalUriReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an external URI reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecurityContextToken/wsp:Policy/sp:SC13SecurityContextToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a Security Context Token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssecureconversation"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-SecureConversation</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This assertion
      does not describe how to obtain a Security Context Token but rather assumes that
      both parties have the token already or have agreed separately on a mechanism for
      obtaining the token. If a definition of the mechanism for obtaining the Security
      Context Token is desired in policy, then either the sp:SecureConversationToken or
      the sp:IssuedToken assertion should be used instead.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826541" name="_Toc161826541"></a><a id="_Toc104103950"
      name="_Toc104103950"></a><a id="_Toc99269251" name="_Toc99269251"></a><a
      id="_SecureConversationToken_Assertion"
      name="_SecureConversationToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.7</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SecureConversationToken
      Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a Security Context Token retrieved from the indicated
      issuer address. If the sp:Issuer address is absent, the protocol MUST be executed
      at the same address as the service endpoint address.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note:</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion describes the token accepted by
      the target service.&nbsp; Because this token is issued by the target service and
      may not have a separate port (with separate policy), this assertion SHOULD contain
      a bootstrap policy indicating the security binding and policy that is used when
      requesting this token from the target service.&nbsp; That is, the bootstrap policy
      is used to obtain the token and then the current (outer) policy is used when making
      requests with the token. This is illustrated in the diagram below.</span></p>
