      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img width="600"
      height="158" src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image002.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SecureConversationToken sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..."
        ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<i>wsa:EndpointReferenceType</i>&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExternalUriReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SC13SecurityContextToken ... /&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustNotSendCancel ... /&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustNotSendAmend ... /&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustNotSendRenew ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:BootstrapPolicy ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:BootstrapPolicy&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SecureConversationToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SecureConversationToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a SecureConversationToken assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains a reference to the issuer for
      the Security Context Token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the
      sp:SecureConversationToken issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SpnegoContextToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the
      sp:SecureConversationToken assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExternalUriReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that an external URI reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:SC13SecurityContextToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a Security Context Token should
      be used as obtained using the protocol defined in [</span><a
      href="#wssecureconversation"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-SecureConversation</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendCancel</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the STS issuing the secure
      conversation token does not support SCT/Cancel RST messages. If this assertion is
      missing it means that SCT/Cancel RST messages are supported by the STS.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendAmend</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the STS issuing the secure
      conversation token does not support SCT/Amend RST messages. If this assertion is
      missing it means that SCT/Amend RST messages are supported by the STS.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:MustNotSendRenew</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the STS issuing the secure
      conversation token does not support SCT/Renew RST messages. If this assertion is
      missing it means that SCT/Renew RST messages are supported by the STS.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:BootstrapPolicy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that contains the policy indicating the requirements
      for obtaining the Security Context Token.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SecureConversationToken/wsp:Policy/sp:BootstrapPolicy/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      contains the security binding requirements for obtaining the Security Context
      Token. It will typically contain a security binding assertion (e.g.
      sp:SymmetricBinding) along with protection assertions (e.g. sp:SignedParts)
      describing the parts of the RST/RSTR messages that are to be protected.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..." xmlns:sp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:SecureConversationToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsa:Address&gt;http://example.org/sts&lt;/wsa:Address&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:SC10SecurityContextToken /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:BootstrapPolicy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:BootstrapPolicy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:SecureConversationToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826542" name="_Toc161826542"></a><a id="_Toc104103951"
      name="_Toc104103951"></a><a id="_SamlToken_Assertion"
      name="_SamlToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.8</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SamlToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a SAML token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SamlToken sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..." ...
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |
        &nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssSamlV11Token10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssSamlV11Token11 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssSamlV20Token11 ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SamlToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SamlToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a SamlToken assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains reference to the issuer of the
      sp:SamlToken.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:SamlToken
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:SamlToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] properties for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a key identifier reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssSamlV11Token10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that identifies that a SAML Version 1.1 token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wsssaml10"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:SAMLTokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssSamlV11Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that identifies that a SAML Version 1.1 token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wsssaml11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:SAMLTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SamlToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssSamlV20Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that identifies that a SAML Version 2.0 token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wsssaml11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:SAMLTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This assertion
      does not describe how to obtain a SAML Token but rather assumes that both parties
      have the token already or have agreed separately on a mechanism for obtaining the
      token. If a definition of the mechanism for obtaining the SAML Token is desired in
      policy, the sp:IssuedToken assertion should be used instead.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826543" name="_Toc161826543"></a><a id="_Toc104103952"
      name="_Toc104103952"></a><a id="_RelToken_Assertion"
      name="_RelToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.9</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>RelToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a REL token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:RelToken sp:IncludeToken="<i>xs:anyURI</i>"? xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |
        &nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssRelV10Token10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssRelV20Token10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssRelV10Token11 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssRelV20Token11 ... /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:RelToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>RelToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a RelToken assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/sp:Issuer</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains reference to the issuer of the
      sp:RelToken.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:RelToken
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:RelToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys], [Explicit Derived Keys]
      and [Implied Derived Keys] property for this token to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireExplicitDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Explicit Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Implied Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireImpliedDerivedKeys</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that sets the [Derived Keys] and [Implied Derived
      Keys] properties for this token to 'true' and the [Explicit Derived Keys] property
      for this token to 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireKeyIdentifierReference</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that a key identifier reference is
      required when referencing this token.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV10Token10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that identifies that a REL Version 1.0 token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssrel10"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:RELTokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV20Token10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that identifies that a REL Version 2.0 token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssrel10"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:RELTokenProfile1.0</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV10Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that identifies that a REL Version 1.0 token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssrel11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:RELTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RelToken/wsp:Policy/sp:WssRelV20Token11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that identifies that a REL Version 2.0 token should
      be used as defined in [</span><a href="#wssrel11"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS:RELTokenProfile1.1</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This assertion
      does not describe how to obtain a REL Token but rather assumes that both parties
      have the token already or have agreed separately on a mechanism for obtaining the
      token. If a definition of the mechanism for obtaining the REL Token is desired in
      policy, the sp:IssuedToken assertion should be used instead.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826544" name="_Toc161826544"></a><a id="_Toc104103953"
      name="_Toc104103953"></a><a id="_HttpsToken_Assertion"
      name="_HttpsToken_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.4.10</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>HttpsToken Assertion</span></h3>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element
      represents a requirement for a transport binding to support the use of
      HTTPS.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:HttpsToken xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;wsa:EndpointReferenceType&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt; |
        &nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuerName&gt;xs:anyURI&lt;/sp:IssuerName&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ) ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wst:Claims Dialect="..."&gt; ... &lt;/wst:Claims&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:HttpBasicAuthentication /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:HttpDigestAuthentication /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireClientCertificate /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; )?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:HttpsToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>HttpsToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>an</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Https</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion stating that use of the HTTPS
      protocol specification is supported</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><a id="_Toc99269252" name="_Toc99269252"></a><a
      id="_Toc96244149" name="_Toc96244149"></a><a id="_Toc95877095"
      name="_Toc95877095"></a><a id="_Toc95113109" name="_Toc95113109"></a><a
      id="_Toc92518201" name="_Toc92518201"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/sp:Issuer</span></a></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type wsa:EndpointReferenceType, contains reference to the issuer of the
      sp:HttpsToken.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/sp:IssuerName</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element, of type xs:anyURI, contains the logical name of the sp:HttpsToken
      issuer.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wst:Claims</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element identifies the required claims that a security token must contain in order
      to satisfy the token assertion requirements.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:HttpsToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy/sp:HttpBasicAuthentication</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the client MUST use HTTP Basic
      Authentication [</span><a href="#rfc2068"><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>RFC2068</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>] to authenticate to the
      service.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy/sp:HttpDigestAuthentication</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the client MUST use HTTP Digest
      Authentication [</span><a href="#rfc2068"><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>RFC2068</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>] to authenticate to the
      service.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:HttpsToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireClientCertificate</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the client MUST provide a
      certificate when negotiating the HTTPS session.</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826545" name="_Toc161826545">5.4.11 KeyValueToken
      Assertion</a></h3>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This element represents a
      requirement for a KeyValue token. The next section defines the KeyValue security
      token abstraction for purposes of this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This document defines
      requirements for KeyValue token when used in combination with RSA cryptographic
      algorithm. Additional cryptographic algorithms can be introduced in other
      specifications by introducing new nested assertions besides
      <i>sp:RsaKeyValue</i>.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:KeyValueToken sp:IncludeToken="xs:anyURI"? xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RsaKeyValue ... /&gt; ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ...</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;/sp:KeyValueToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Default"><span style='font-size:10.0pt'>The following describes the
      attributes listed in the schema outlined above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a RsaToken assertion</span>.</p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken/@sp:IncludeToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      attribute identifies the token inclusion value for this token assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element identifies additional requirements for use of the sp:KeyValueToken
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:KeyValueToken/wsp:Policy/sp:RsaKeyValue</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the ds:RSAKeyValue element must
      be present in the KeyValue token. This indicates that an RSA key pair must be
      used.</span></p>
      <h4>5.4.11.1 KeyValue Token</h4>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>XML Signature
      specification allows reference an arbitrary key pair by using the corresponding
      public key value. This allows using an arbitrary key pair to sign or encrypt XML
      elements. The purpose of this section is to define the KeyValue token abstraction
      that represents such key pair referencing mechanism.</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Although the
      <i>ds:KeyValue</i> element as defined in the XML Signature specification is generic
      enough to be used with any asymmetric cryptographic algorithm this document only
      profiles the usage of <i>ds:KeyValue</i> element in combination with RSA
      cryptographic algorithm.</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The RSA key pair is
      represented by the <i>ds:KeyInfo</i> element containing the <i>ds:KeyValue</i>
      element with the RSA public key value in <i>ds:RSAKeyValue</i> as defined in the
      XML Signature specification:</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;ds:KeyInfo xmlns="http://www.w3/org/2000/09/xmldsig#"&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyValue&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:RSAKeyValue&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Modulus&gt;<i>ds:CryptoBinary</i>&lt;/ds:Modulus&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Exponent&gt;<i>ds:CryptoBinary</i>&lt;/ds:Exponent&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:RSAKeyValue&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyValue&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When the KeyValue token
      is used the corresponding public key value appears directly in the signature or
      encrypted data <i>ds:KeyInfo</i> element like in the following example. There is no
      KeyValue token manifestation outside the <i>ds:KeyInfo</i> element.</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;Signature xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#"&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;SignedInfo&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;CanonicalizationMethod
        Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2001/10/xml-exc-c14n#" /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;SignatureMethod
        Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#rsa-sha1" /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;Reference URI="#_1"&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;Transforms&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;Transform
        Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2001/10/xml-exc-c14n#" /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/Transforms&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;DigestMethod
        Algorithm="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#sha1" /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;DigestValue&gt;...&lt;/DigestValue&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/Reference&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;/SignedInfo&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/SignatureValue&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp; &lt;KeyInfo&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;KeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;RSAKeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;Modulus&gt;...&lt;/Modulus&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;Exponent&gt;...&lt;/Exponent&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/RSAKeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/KeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp; &lt;/KeyInfo&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;/Signature&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Default"><span
      style='font-size:10.0pt;font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Since there is no
      representation of the KeyValue token outside the <i>ds:KeyInfo</i> element and thus
      no identifier can be associated with the token, the KeyValue token cannot be
      referenced by using <i>wsse:SecurityTokenReference</i> element. However the
      <i>ds:KeyInfo</i> element representing the KeyValue token can be used whenever a
      security token can be used as illustrated on the following example:</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;t:RequestSecurityToken xmlns:t="..."&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;t:RequestType&gt;...&lt;/t:RequestType&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ...</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;t:UseKey&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; <i>&lt;KeyInfo
        xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/09/xmldsig#"&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;KeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;RSAKeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;Modulus&gt;...&lt;/Modulus&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;Exponent&gt;...&lt;/Exponent&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/RSAKeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/KeyValue&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <i>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/KeyInfo&gt;</i></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;/t:UseKey&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;/t:RequestSecurityToken&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc99269255" name="_Toc99269255"></a><a id="_Toc161826546"
        name="_Toc161826546"></a><a id="_Toc104103954" name="_Toc104103954"><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
        <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Security Binding
        Properties</span></a></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section defines
      the various properties or conditions of a security binding, their semantics, values
      and defaults where appropriate. Properties are used by a binding in a manner
      similar to how variables are used in code. Assertions populate, (or set) the value
      of the property (or variable). When an assertion that populates a value of a
      property appears in a policy, that property is set to the value indicated by the
      assertion. The security binding then uses the value of the property to control its
      behavior. The properties listed here are common to the various security bindings
      described in Section 7. Assertions that define values for these properties are
      defined in Section 7. The following properties are used by the security binding
      assertions.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc96244152" name="_Toc96244152"></a><a id="_Toc95877098"
      name="_Toc95877098"></a><a id="_Toc95113095" name="_Toc95113095"></a><a
      id="_Toc85362766" name="_Toc85362766"></a><a id="_Toc85350212"
      name="_Toc85350212"></a><a id="_Toc161826547" name="_Toc161826547"></a><a
      id="_Toc104103955" name="_Toc104103955"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.1</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Algorithm Suite] Property</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
      specifies the algorithm suite required for performing cryptographic operations with
      symmetric or asymmetric key based security tokens. An algorithm suite specifies
      actual algorithms and allowed key lengths. A policy alternative will define what
      algorithms are used and how they are used. This property defines the set of
      available algorithms. The value of this property is typically referenced by a
      security binding and is used to specify the algorithms used for all message level
      cryptographic operations performed under the security binding.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: In some cases,
      this property MAY be referenced under a context other than a security binding and
      used to control the algorithms used under that context. For example, supporting
      token assertions define such a context. In such contexts, the specified algorithms
      still apply to message level cryptographic operations.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>An algorithm suite
      defines values for each of the following operations and properties:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Sym
      Sig]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Symmetric Key
      Signature</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Asym
      Sig]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Signature with an asymmetric
      key</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Dig]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      Digest</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Enc]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      Encryption</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Sym
      KW]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Symmetric Key Wrap</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Asym
      KW]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Asymmetric Key Wrap</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Comp
      Key]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Computed key</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Enc
      KD]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Encryption key
      derivation</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Sig
      KD]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Signature key
      derivation</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Min
      SKL]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Minimum symmetric key
      length</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Max
      SKL]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Maximum symmetric key
      length</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Min
      AKL]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Minimum asymmetric key
      length</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Max
      AKL]&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; Maximum asymmetric key
      length</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following table
      provides abbreviations for the algorithm URI used in the table below:</span></p>
      <table class="MsoNormalTable" border="0" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0"

      <h2><a id="_Toc96244153" name="_Toc96244153"></a><a id="_Toc95877099"
      name="_Toc95877099"></a><a id="_Toc95113096" name="_Toc95113096"></a><a
      id="_Toc85362767" name="_Toc85362767"></a><a id="_Toc85350213"
      name="_Toc85350213"></a><a id="_Toc161826548" name="_Toc161826548"></a><a
      id="_Toc104103956" name="_Toc104103956"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Timestamp] Property</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether a</span> <span class="CodeChar"><span
      style='font-size:9.0pt'>wsu:Timestamp</span></span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>element is present in the</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Courier New"'>wsse:Security</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>header. If the value is 'true', the
      timestamp element MUST be present and MUST be integrity protected either by
      transport or message level security. If the value is 'false', the timestamp element
      MUST NOT be present. The default value for this property is 'false'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826549" name="_Toc161826549"></a><a id="_Toc104103957"
      name="_Toc104103957"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>6.3</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Protection Order]
      Property</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
      indicates the order in which integrity and confidentiality are applied to the
      message, in cases where both integrity and confidentiality are required:</span></p>
      <table class="MsoNormalTable" border="1" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0"
      style='border-collapse:collapse;border:none'>
        <tr>
          <td width="253" valign="top"
          style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><a id="_Toc99269262" name="_Toc99269262"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>EncryptBeforeSigning</span></a></p>
          </td>
          <td width="307" valign="top"
          style='width:3.2in;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-left:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signature MUST
            computed over ciphertext. Encryption key and signing key MUST be derived from
            the same source key unless distinct keys are provided, see Section 7.5 on the
            AsymmetricBinding.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="253" valign="top"
          style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SignBeforeEncrypting</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="307" valign="top"
          style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none; border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signature MUST
            be computed over plaintext. The resulting signature SHOULD be encrypted.
            Supporting signatures MUST be over the plain text signature.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
      </table>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The default value
      for this property is 'SignBeforeEncrypting'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826550" name="_Toc161826550"></a><a id="_Toc104103958"
      name="_Toc104103958"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>6.4</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Signature Protection]
      Property</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether the signature must be encrypted. If the value is 'true',
      the primary signature MUST be encrypted and any signature confirmation elements
      MUST also be encrypted. The primary signature element is not required to be
      encrypted if the value is &lsquo;true&rsquo; when there is nothing else in the
      message that is encrypted. If the value is 'false', the primary signature MUST NOT
      be encrypted and any signature confirmation elements MUST NOT be encrypted. The
      default value for this property is 'false'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826551" name="_Toc161826551"></a><a id="_Toc104103959"
      name="_Toc104103959"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>6.5</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Token Protection]
      Property</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether signatures must cover the token used to generate that
      signature. If the value is 'true', then each token used to generate a signature
      MUST be covered by that signature. If the value is 'false', then the token MUST NOT
      be covered by the signature. Note that in cases where derived keys are used the
      'main' token, and NOT the derived key token, is covered by the signature. It is
      recommended that assertions that define values for this property apply to [Endpoint
      Policy Subject]. The default value for this property is 'false'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826552" name="_Toc161826552"></a><a id="_Toc104103960"
      name="_Toc104103960"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>6.6</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Entire Header and Body Signatures]
      Property</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether signature digests over the SOAP body and SOAP headers
      must only cover the entire body and entire header elements. If the value is 'true',
      then each digest over the SOAP body MUST be over the entire SOAP body element and
      not a descendant of that element. In addition each digest over a SOAP header MUST
      be over an actual header element and not a descendant of a header element. This
      restriction does not specifically apply to the wsse:Security header. However
      signature digests over child elements of the wsse:Security header MUST be over the
      entire child element and not a descendent of that element. If the value is 'false',
      then signature digests MAY be over a descendant of the SOAP Body or a descendant of
      a header element. Setting the value of this property to 'true' mitigates against
      some possible re-writing attacks. It is recommended that assertions that define
      values for this property apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject]. The default value for
      this property is 'false'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826553" name="_Toc161826553"></a><a id="_Toc104103961"
      name="_Toc104103961"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>6.7</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Security Header Layout]
      Property</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
      indicates which layout rules to apply when adding items to the security header. The
      following table shows which rules are defined by this specification.</span></p>
      <table class="MsoNormalTable" border="1" cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0"
      style='border-collapse:collapse;border:none'>
        <tr>
          <td width="253" valign="top"
          style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Strict</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="307" valign="top"
          style='width:3.2in;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-left:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Items are
            added to the security header following the numbered layout rules described
            below according to a general principle of 'declare before use'.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="253" valign="top"
          style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Lax</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="307" valign="top"
          style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none; border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Items are
            added to the security header in any order that conforms to WSS: SOAP Message
            Security</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="253" valign="top"
          style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>LaxTimestampFirst</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="307" valign="top"
          style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none; border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>As Lax except
            that the first item in the security header MUST be a wsse:Timestamp.</span>
            <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note that the [Timestamp]
            property MUST also be set to 'true' in this case.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
          <td width="253" valign="top"
          style='width:190.0pt;border:solid windowtext 1.0pt; border-top:none;padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="MsoNormal"><span
            style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>LaxTimestampLast</span></p>
          </td>
          <td width="307" valign="top"
          style='width:3.2in;border-top:none;border-left:none; border-bottom:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:solid windowtext 1.0pt; padding:0in 5.4pt 0in 5.4pt'>
            <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>As Lax except
            that the last item in the security header MUST be a wsse:Timestamp.</span>
            <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note that the [Timestamp]
            property MUST also be set to 'true' in this case.</span></p>
          </td>
        </tr>
      </table>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <h3><a id="_Toc161826554" name="_Toc161826554"></a><a id="_Toc104103962"
      name="_Toc104103962"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>6.7.1</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Strict Layout Rules</span></a> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>for WSS 1.0</span></h3>
      <ol style='margin-top:0in' start="1" type="1">
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Tokens that
        are included in the message MUST be declared before use. For example:</span></li>
        <li style="list-style: none">
          <ol style='margin-top:0in' start="1" type="a">
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A local
            signing token MUST occur before the signature that uses it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A local
            token serving as the source token for a derived key token MUST occur before
            that derived key token.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A local
            encryption token MUST occur before the reference list that points to
            xenc:EncryptedData elements that use it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the
            same token is used for both signing and encryption, then it should appear
            before the ds:Signature and xenc:ReferenceList elements in the security
            header that are generated using the token.</span></li>
          </ol>
        </li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed
        elements inside the security header MUST occur before the signature that signs
        them.&nbsp; For example:</span></li>
        <li style="list-style: none">
          <ol style='margin-top:0in' start="1" type="a">
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
            timestamp MUST occur before the signature that signs it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
            Username token (usually in encrypted form) MUST occur before the signature
            that signs it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
            primary signature MUST occur before the supporting token signature that signs
            the primary signature's signature value element.</span></li>
          </ol>
        </li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When an
        element in a security header is encrypted, the resulting xenc:EncryptedData
        element has the same order requirements as the source plain text element, unless
        requirement 4 indicates otherwise. For example, an encrypted primary signature
        MUST occur before any supporting token signature per 2.c above and an encrypted
        token has the same ordering requirements as the unencrypted token.</span></li>
      </ol>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If there are
      any encrypted elements in the message then a top level xenc:ReferenceList element
      or a top level xenc:EncryptedKey element which contains an xenc:ReferenceList
      element MUST be present in the security header. The xenc:ReferenceList or
      xenc:EncryptedKey MUST occur before any xenc:EncryptedData elements in the security
      header that are referenced from the reference list. Strict Layout Rules for WSS
      1.1</span></p>
      <ol style='margin-top:0in' start="1" type="1">
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Tokens that
        are included in the message MUST be declared before use. For example:</span></li>
        <li style="list-style: none">
          <ol style='margin-top:0in' start="1" type="a">
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A local
            signing token MUST occur before the signature that uses it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A local
            token serving as the source token for a derived key token MUST occur before
            that derived key token.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A local
            encryption token MUST occur before the reference list that points to
            xenc:EncryptedData elements that use it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the
            same token is used for both signing and encryption, then it should appear
            before the ds:Signature and xenc:ReferenceList elements in the security
            header that are generated using the token.</span></li>
          </ol>
        </li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed
        elements inside the security header MUST occur before the signature that signs
        them.&nbsp; For example:</span></li>
        <li style="list-style: none">
          <ol style='margin-top:0in' start="1" type="a">
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
            timestamp MUST occur before the signature that signs it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
            Username token (usually in encrypted form) MUST occur before the signature
            that signs it.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
            primary signature MUST occur before the supporting token signature that signs
            the primary signature's signature value element.</span></li>
            <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A
            wsse11:SignatureConfirmation element MUST occur before the signature that
            signs it.</span></li>
          </ol>
        </li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When an
        element in a security header is encrypted, the resulting xenc:EncryptedData
        element has the same order requirements as the source plain text element, unless
        requirement 4 indicates otherwise. For example, an encrypted primary signature
        MUST occur before any supporting token signature per 2.c above and an encrypted
        token has the same ordering requirements as the unencrypted token.</span></li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If there are
        any encrypted elements in the message then a top level xenc:ReferenceList element
        MUST be present in the security header. The xenc:ReferenceList MUST occur before
        any xenc:EncryptedData elements in the security header that are referenced from
        the reference list. However, the xenc:ReferenceList is not required to appear
        before independently encrypted tokens such as the xenc:EncryptedKey token as
        defined in WSS.</span></li>
        <li class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>An
        xenc:EncryptedKey element without an internal reference list [</span><a
        href="#wss11"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP Message
        Security 1.1</span></a><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>] MUST obey
        rule 1 above.</span></li>
      </ol>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc161826555" name="_Toc161826555"></a><a id="_Toc104103963"
        name="_Toc104103963"></a><a id="_Toc96244166" name="_Toc96244166"></a><a
