        id="_Toc95877112" name="_Toc95877112"></a><a id="_Toc95113117"
        name="_Toc95113117"></a><a id="_Toc85362795" name="_Toc85362795"></a><a
        id="_Toc85350240" name="_Toc85350240"><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
        <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Security Binding
        Assertions</span></a></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The appropriate
      representation of the different facets of security mechanisms requires distilling
      the common primitives (to enable reuse) and then combining the primitive elements
      into patterns. The policy scope of assertions defined in this section is the policy
      scope of their containing element.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc96244167" name="_Toc96244167"></a><a id="_Toc95877113"
      name="_Toc95877113"></a><a id="_Toc95113118" name="_Toc95113118"></a><a
      id="_Toc85362796" name="_Toc85362796"></a><a id="_Toc85350241"
      name="_Toc85350241"></a><a id="_Toc73939793" name="_Toc73939793"></a><a
      id="_Toc161826556" name="_Toc161826556"></a><a id="_Toc104103964"
      name="_Toc104103964"></a><a id="_AlgorithmSuite_Assertion"
      name="_AlgorithmSuite_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.1</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AlgorithmSuite Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This assertion
      indicates a requirement for an algorithm suite as defined under the [Algorithm
      Suite] property described in Section 6.1. The scope of this assertion is defined by
      its containing assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp; (&lt;sp:Basic256 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic192 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic128 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:TripleDes ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic192Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic128Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:TripleDesRsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic256Sha256 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic192Sha256 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic128Sha256 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:TripleDesSha256 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic256Sha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic192Sha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic128Sha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:TripleDesSha256Rsa15 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...)<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:InclusiveC14N ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SOAPNormalization10 ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:STRTransform10 ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp; (&lt;sp:XPath10 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:XPathFilter20 ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AbsXPath ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...)?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>n AlgorithmSuite</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element contains one or more policy assertions that indicate the specific algorithm
      suite to use.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic256'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic192'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic128'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDes</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'TripleDes'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256Rsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic256Rsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192Rsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic192Rsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128Rsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic128Rsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDesRsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'TripleDesRsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256Sha256</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic256Sha256'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192Sha256</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic192Sha256'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128Sha256</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic128Sha256'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDesSha256</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'TripleDesSha256'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic256Sha256Rsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic256Sha256Rsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic192Sha256Rsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic192Sha256Rsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:Basic128Sha256Rsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'Basic128Sha256Rsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:TripleDesSha256Rsa15</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Algorithm Suite] property is
      set to 'TripleDesSha256Rsa15'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:InclusiveC14N</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [C14N] property of an
      algorithm suite is set to 'C14N'. Note: as indicated in Section 6.1 the default
      value of the [C14N] property is 'ExcC14N'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:SoapNormalization10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [SOAP Norm] property is set
      to 'SNT'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:STRTransform10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [STR Transform] property is
      set to 'STRT10'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:XPath10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [XPath] property is set to
      'XPath'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:XPathFilter20</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [XPath] property is set to
      'XPath20'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite/wsp:Policy/sp:AbsXPath</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [XPath] property is set to
      'AbsXPath' (see</span> <a
      href="http://www.w3.org/TR/1999/REC-xpath-19991116#NT-AbsoluteLocationPath"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AbsoluteLocationPath</span></a> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>in [</span><a href="#xpath"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>XPATH</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>]).</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826557" name="_Toc161826557"></a><a id="_Toc104103965"
      name="_Toc104103965"></a><a id="_Layout_Assertion"
      name="_Layout_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Layout</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This assertion
      indicates a requirement for a particular security header layout as defined under
      the [Security Header Layout] property described in Section 6.7. The scope of this
      assertion is defined by its containing assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:Layout xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Strict ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Lax ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:LaxTsFirst ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:LaxTsLast ... /&gt; |<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:Layout&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Layout</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Layout</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal" style='margin-left:.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required element contains one or more
      policy assertions that indicate the specific security header layout to
      use.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:Strict</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Security Header Layout]
      property is set to 'Strict'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:Lax</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Security Header Layout]
      property is set to 'Lax'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:LaxTsFirst</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Security Header Layout]
      property is set to 'LaxTimestampFirst'. Note that the [Timestamp] property MUST
      also be set to 'true' by the presence of an sp:IncludeTimestamp
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout/wsp:Policy/sp:LaxTsLast</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Security Header Layout]
      property is set to 'LaxTimestampLast'. Note that the [Timestamp] property MUST also
      be set to 'true' by the presence of an sp:IncludeTimestamp assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826558" name="_Toc161826558"></a><a id="_Toc104103966"
      name="_Toc104103966"></a><a id="_TransportBinding_Assertion"
      name="_TransportBinding_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.3</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>TransportBinding</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The TransportBinding
      assertion is used in scenarios in which message protection and security correlation
      is provided by means other than</span> <a href="#wssecurity"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP Message Security</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>, for example by a secure transport like
      HTTPS.&nbsp; Specifically, this assertion indicates that the message is protected
      using the means provided by the transport. This binding has one binding specific
      token property; [Transport Token]. This assertion MUST apply to [Endpoint Policy
      Subject].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:TransportBinding xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:TransportToken ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:TransportToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Layout ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:Layout&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:TransportBinding&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>TransportBinding</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a TransportBinding assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This indicates
      a nested</span> <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsp:Policy</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element that defines the behavior of the
      TransportBinding assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:TransportToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for a Transport Token.
      The specified token populates the [Transport Token] property and indicates how the
      transport is secured.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:TransportToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This indicates
      a nested policy that identifies the type of Transport Token to use.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a value that populates the [Algorithm
      Suite] property. See Section 6.1 for more details.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:Layout</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a value that populates the [Security
      Header Layout] property. See Section 6.7 for more details.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:TransportBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:IncludeTimestamp</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Timestamp] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826559" name="_Toc161826559"></a><a id="_Toc104103967"
      name="_Toc104103967"></a><a id="_SymmetricBinding_Assertion"
      name="_SymmetricBinding_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.4</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The SymmetricBinding
      assertion is used in scenarios in which message protection is provided by means
      defined in</span> <a href="#wssecurity"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP Message Security</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>. This binding has two binding specific
      token properties; [Encryption Token] and [Signature Token]. If the message pattern
      requires multiple messages, this binding defines that the [Encryption Token] used
      from initiator to recipient is also used from recipient to initiator. Similarly,
      the [Signature Token] used from initiator to recipient is also use from recipient
      to initiator. If a sp:ProtectionToken assertion is specified, the specified token
      populates both token properties and is used as the basis for both encryption and
      signature in both directions. This assertion SHOULD apply to [Endpoint Policy
      Subject]. This assertion MAY apply to [Operation Policy Subject].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SymmetricBinding xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptionToken ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:EncryptionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignatureToken ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignatureToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) | (<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectionToken ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; )<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Layout ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:Layout&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptSignature ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectTokens ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SymmetricBinding</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a SymmetricBinding assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This indicates
      a nested wsp:Policy element that defines the behavior of the SymmetricBinding
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptionToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for an Encryption Token.
      The specified token populates the [Encryption Token] property and is used for
      encryption. It is an error for both an sp:EncryptionToken and an sp:ProtectionToken
      assertion to be specified.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptionToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify exactly one token to use for encryption.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SignatureToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for a Signature Token.
      The specified token populates the [Signature Token] property and is used for the
      message signature. It is an error for both an sp:SignatureToken and an
      sp:ProtectionToken assertion to be specified.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignatureToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify exactly one token to use for signatures.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:ProtectionToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for a Protection Token.
      The specified token populates the [Encryption Token] and [Signature Token
      properties] and is used for the message signature and for encryption. It is an
      error for both an sp:ProtectionToken assertion and either an sp:EncryptionToken
      assertion or an sp:SignatureToken assertion to be specified.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:ProtectionToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify exactly one token to use for protection.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a value that populates the [Algorithm
      Suite] property. See Section 6.1 for more details.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Layout</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a value that populates the [Security
      Header Layout] property. See Section 6.7 for more details.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:IncludeTimestamp</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Timestamp] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptBeforeSigning</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Protection Order] property
      is set to 'EncryptBeforeSigning'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EncryptSignature</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Signature Protection]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:ProtectTokens</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Token Protection] property
      is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><a id="_Toc96244172" name="_Toc96244172"></a><a id="_Toc95877118"
      name="_Toc95877118"></a><a id="_Toc95113123" name="_Toc95113123"></a><a
      id="_Toc85362811" name="_Toc85362811"></a><a id="_Toc85350256"
      name="_Toc85350256"></a><a id="_Toc73939808" name="_Toc73939808"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SymmetricBinding</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Entire Header And Body
      Signatures] property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826560" name="_Toc161826560"></a><a id="_Toc104103968"
      name="_Toc104103968"></a><a id="_AsymmetricBinding_Assertion"
      name="_AsymmetricBinding_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.5</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
      AsymmetricBinding assertion is used in scenarios in which message protection is
      provided by means defined in WSS: SOAP Message Security using asymmetric key
      (Public Key) technology. Commonly used asymmetric algorithms, such as RSA, allow
      the same key pair to be used for both encryption and signature. However it is also
      common practice to use distinct keys for encryption and signature, because of their
      different lifecycles.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This binding enables
      either of these practices by means of four binding specific token properties:
      [Initiator Signature Token], [Initiator Encryption Token], [Recipient Signature
      Token] and [Recipient Encryption Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the same key pair
      is used for signature and encryption, then [Initiator Signature Token] and
      [Initiator Encryption Token] will both refer to the same token. Likewise [Recipient
      Signature Token] and [Recipient Encryption Token] will both refer to the same
      token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If distinct key
      pairs are used for signature and encryption then [Initiator Signature Token] and
      [Initiator Encryption Token] will refer to different tokens. Likewise [Recipient
      Signature Token] and [Recipient Encryption Token] will refer to different
      tokens.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the message
      pattern requires multiple messages, the [Initiator Signature Token] is used for the
      message signature from initiator to the recipient. The [Initiator Encryption Token]
      is used for the response message encryption from recipient to the initiator. The
      [Recipient Signature Token] is used for the response message signature from
      recipient to the initiator. The [Recipient Encryption Token] is used for the
      message encryption from initiator to the recipient. Note that in each case, the
      token is associated with the party (initiator or recipient) who knows the
      secret.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This assertion
      SHOULD apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject]. This assertion MAY apply to [Operation
      Policy Subject].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:AsymmetricBinding xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ... &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/sp:InitiatorToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) | (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:InitiatorSignatureToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/sp:InitiatorSignatureToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; )</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/sp:RecipientToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) | (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RecipientSignatureToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/sp:RecipientSignatureToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;sp:RecipientEncryptionToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt; ...
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/sp:RecipientEncryptionToken&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; )<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Layout ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:Layout&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptSignature ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectTokens ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:AsymmetricBinding</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a AsymmetricBinding assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This indicates
      a nested wsp:Policy element that defines the behavior of the AsymmetricBinding
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:InitiatorToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for an Initiator Token.
      The specified token populates the [Initiator Signature Token] and [Initiator
      Encryption Token] properties and is used for the message signature from initiator
      to recipient, and encryption from recipient to initiator.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
      class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorSignatureToken</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for an Initiator
      Signature Token. The specified token populates the [Initiator Signature Token]
      property and is used for the message signature from initiator to
      recipient.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; letter-spacing:-.6pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.6pt'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
       class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorSignatureToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; letter-spacing:-.2pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.2pt'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
       class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for an Initiator
      Encryption Token. The specified token populates the [Initiator Encryption Token]
      property and is used for the message encryption from recipient to
      initiator.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; letter-spacing:-.6pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.6pt'>sp:InitiatorToken/wsp:Policy<span
       class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:InitiatorEncryptionToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:RecipientToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for a Recipient Token.
      The specified token populates the [Recipient Signature Token] and [Recipient
      Encryption Token] property and is used for encryption from initiator to recipient,
      and for the message signature from recipient to initiator.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; letter-spacing:-.3pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.3pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
       class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientSignatureToken</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for a Recipient
      Signature Token. The specified token populates the [Recipient Signature Token]
      property and is used for the message signature from Recipient to
      recipient.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; letter-spacing:-.7pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.7pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
       class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientSignatureToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; letter-spacing:-.3pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.3pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
       class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientEncryptionToken</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a requirement for a Recipient
      Encryption Token. The specified token populates the [Recipient Encryption Token]
      property and is used for the message encryption from recipient to
      Recipient.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"; letter-spacing:-.7pt'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/</span></span><span
       style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif";letter-spacing:-.7pt'>sp:RecipientToken/wsp:Policy<span
       class="TextChar1"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:RecipientEncryptionToken/wsp:Policy</span></span></span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      contained here MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AsymmetricBinding</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This required
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a value that populates the [Algorithm
      Suite] property. See Section 6.1 for more details.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:Layout</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates a value that populates the [Security
      Header Layout] property. See Section 6.7 for more details.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:IncludeTimestamp</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Timestamp] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptBeforeSigning</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Protection Order] property
      is set to 'EncryptBeforeSigning'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptSignature</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Signature Protection]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding/wsp:Policy/sp:ProtectTokens</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Token Protection] property
      is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><a id="_Toc96244187" name="_Toc96244187"></a><a id="_Toc95877133"
      name="_Toc95877133"></a><a id="_Toc95113138" name="_Toc95113138"></a><a
      id="_Toc85362846" name="_Toc85362846"></a><a id="_Toc85350291"
      name="_Toc85350291"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:AsymmetricBinding</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/wsp:Policy</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Entire Header And Body
      Signatures] property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc161826561" name="_Toc161826561"></a><a id="_Toc104103969"
        name="_Toc104103969"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>8<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
        <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Supporting Tokens</span></a></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Security Bindings
      use tokens to secure the message exchange. The Security Binding will require one to
      create a signature using the token identified in the Security Binding policy. This
      signature will here-to-fore be referred to as the &ldquo;message signature&rdquo;.
      In case of Transport Binding the message is signed outside of the message XML by
      the underlying transport protocol and the signature itself is not part of the
      message. Additional tokens may be specified to augment the claims provided by the
      token associated with the &ldquo;message signature&rdquo; provided by the Security
      Binding. This section defines seven properties related to supporting token
      requirements which may be referenced by a Security Binding: [Supporting Tokens],
      [Signed Supporting Tokens], [Endorsing Supporting Tokens], [Signed Endorsing
      Supporting Tokens], [Signed Encrypted Supporting Tokens], [Endorsing Encrypted
      Supporting Tokens] and [Signed Endorsing Encrypted Supporting Tokens]. Seven
      assertions are defined to populate those properties: SupportingTokens,
      SignedSupportingTokens, EndorsingSupportingTokens, SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens,
      SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens, EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens and
      SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens.</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>These assertions SHOULD apply to [Endpoint
      Policy Subject]. These assertions MAY apply to [Message Policy Subject] or
      [Operation Policy Subject].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Supporting tokens
      may be specified at a different scope than the binding assertion which provides
      support for securing the exchange. For instance, a binding is specified at the
      scope of an endpoint, while the supporting tokens might be defined at the scope of
      a message. When assertions that populate this property are defined in overlapping
      scopes, the sender should merge the requirements by including all tokens from the
      outer scope and any additional tokens for a specific message from the inner
      scope.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>In cases where
      multiple tokens are specified that sign and/or encrypt overlapping message parts,
      all the tokens should sign and encrypt the various message parts. In such cases
      ordering of elements (tokens, signatures, reference lists etc.) in the security
      header would be used to determine which order signature and encryptions occurred
      in.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Policy authors need
      to ensure that the tokens they specify as supporting tokens can satisfy any
      additional constraints defined by the supporting token assertion. For example, if
      the supporting token assertion specifies message parts that need to be encrypted,
      the specified tokens need to be capable of encryption.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>To illustrate the
      different ways that supporting tokens may be bound to the message, let&rsquo;s
      consider a message with three components: Header1, Header2, and Body.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="125"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image003.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Even before any
      supporting tokens are added, each binding requires that the message is signed using
      a token satisfying the required usage for that binding, and that the signature
      (Sig1) covers important parts of the message including the message timestamp (TS)
      facilitate replay detection. The signature is then included as part of the Security
      header as illustrated below:</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="209"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image004.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: if required,
      the initiator may also include in the Security header the token used as the basis
      for the message signature (Sig1), not shown in the diagram.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
      security is used, only the message timestamp (TS) is included in the Security
      header as illustrated below. The &ldquo;message signature&rdquo; is provided by the
      underlying transport protocol and is not part of the message XML.</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="185"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image005.gif" /></span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826562" name="_Toc161826562"></a><a id="_Toc104103970"
      name="_Toc104103970"></a><a id="_SupportingTokens_Assertion"
      name="_SupportingTokens_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.1</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SupportingTokens Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Supporting tokens
      are included in the security header and may optionally include additional message
      parts to sign and/or encrypt. The supporting tokens can be added to any SOAP
      message and do not require any protection (signature or encryption) to be applied
      to the message before they are added. More specifically there is no requirement on
      &ldquo;message signature&rdquo; being present before the supporting tokens are
      added. However it is RECOMMENDED to employ underlying protection mechanism to
      ensure that the supporting tokens are cryptographically bound to the message during
      the transmission.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SupportingTokens xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; [Token Assertion]+</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;
        ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span xml:lang="DA">&gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span> &lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt;
        ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) *</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SupportingTokens&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SupportingTokens</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a SupportingTokens assertion. The specified tokens populate the
      [Supporting Tokens] property.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This describes
      additional requirements for satisfying the SupportingTokens assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
      Assertion]</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and
      describes the algorithms to use for cryptographic operations performed with the
      tokens identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be included in the signature generated
      with the token identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.2 and
      describes additional message elements that MUST be included in the signature
      generated with the token identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and
      describes additional message elements that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826563" name="_Toc161826563"></a><a id="_Toc104103971"
      name="_Toc104103971"></a><a id="_SignedSupportingTokens_Assertion"
      name="_SignedSupportingTokens_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SignedSupportingTokens
      Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed tokens are
      included in the &ldquo;message signature&rdquo; as defined above</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>and may optionally include additional
      message parts to sign and/or encrypt</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>. The diagram below illustrates how the
      attached token (Tok2) is signed by the message signature (Sig1):</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="233"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image006.gif" />&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
      security is used, the token (Tok2) is included in the Security header as
      illustrated below:</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="209"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image007.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; [Token Assertion]+</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;
        ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span xml:lang="DA">&gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span> &lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt;
        ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) *</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SignedSupportingTokens</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a SignedSupportingTokens assertion. The specified tokens populate the
      [Signed Supporting Tokens] property.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This describes
      additional requirements for satisfying the SignedSupportingTokens
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
      Assertion]</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and
      describes the algorithms to use for cryptographic operations performed with the
      tokens identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be included in the signature generated
      with the token identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.2 and
      describes additional message elements that MUST be included in the signature
      generated with the token identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and
      describes additional message elements that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826564" name="_Toc161826564"></a><a id="_Toc104103972"
      name="_Toc104103972"></a><a id="_EndorsingSupportingTokens_Assertion"
      name="_EndorsingSupportingTokens_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.3</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>EndorsingSupportingTokens
      Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Endorsing tokens
      sign the message signature, that is they sign the entire</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>ds:Signature</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>element produced from the message
      signature</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>and may optionally
      include additional message parts to sign and/or encrypt</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>. The diagram below illustrates how the
      endorsing signature (Sig2) signs the message signature (Sig1):</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="233"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image008.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
      security is used, the signature (Sig2) MUST cover the message timestamp as
      illustrated below:</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="209"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image009.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; [Token Assertion]+</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;
        ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span xml:lang="DA">&gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span> &lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt;
        ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) *</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>EndorsingSupportingTokens</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies an EndorsingSupportingTokens assertion. The specified tokens populate
      the [Endorsing Supporting Tokens] property.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This describes
      additional requirements for satisfying the EndorsingSupportingTokens
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
