      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
      Assertion]</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and
      describes the algorithms to use for cryptographic operations performed with the
      tokens identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be included in the signature generated
      with the token identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.2 and
      describes additional message elements that MUST be included in the signature
      generated with the token identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and
      describes additional message elements that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc104103973" name="_Toc104103973"></a><a
      id="_SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens_Ass"
      name="_SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens_Ass"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.4</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;<a id="_Toc161826565"
      name="_Toc161826565">SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens Assertion</a></span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed endorsing
      tokens sign the entire</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>ds:Signature</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element produced from the message
      signature and are themselves signed by that message signature, that is both tokens
      (the token used for the message signature and the signed endorsing token) sign each
      other. This assertion</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>may
      optionally include additional message parts to sign and/or encrypt.</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The diagram below illustrates how the
      signed token (Tok2) is signed by the message signature (Sig1) and the endorsing
      signature (Sig2) signs the message signature (Sig1):</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="257"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image010.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If transport
      security is used, the token (Tok2) is included in the Security header and the
      signature (Sig2) should cover the message timestamp as illustrated
      below:</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><i><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="197" height="233"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image011.gif" /></span></i></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; [Token Assertion]+</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite ... &gt; ... &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;
        ?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedParts ... <span xml:lang="DA">&gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:SignedElements&gt; |</span></p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        xml:lang="DA">&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span> &lt;sp:EncryptedParts ... &gt;
        ... &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt; |</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptedElements ... &gt; ...
        &lt;/sp:EncryptedElements&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ) *</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens assertion. The specified tokens
      populate the [Signed Endorsing Supporting Tokens] property.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><a id="_Toc99269276" name="_Toc99269276"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy</span></a></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This describes
      additional requirements for satisfying the EndorsingSupportingTokens
      assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/[Token
      Assertion]</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The policy
      MUST identify one or more token assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:AlgorithmSuite</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 7.1 and
      describes the algorithms to use for cryptographic operations performed with the
      tokens identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be included in the signature generated
      with the token identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:SignedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element follows the schema outlined in Section 4.1.2 and describes additional
      message elements that MUST be included in the signature generated with the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedParts</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.1 and
      describes additional message parts that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens/wsp:Policy/sp:EncryptedElements</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that follows the schema outlined in Section 4.2.2 and
      describes additional message elements that MUST be encrypted using the token
      identified by this policy assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826566" name="_Toc161826566"></a><a
      id="_SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens_Ass"
      name="_SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens_Ass"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.5</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens
      Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed, encrypted
      supporting tokens are Signed supporting tokens (See</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>section 8.2) that are also encrypted when
      they appear in the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>wsse:SecurityHeader. Element Encryption
      SHOULD be used for encrypting the supporting tokens.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The syntax for the
      sp:SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens differs from the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>syntax of sp:SignedSupportingTokens only
      in the name of the assertion</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>itself. All nested policy is as per the
      sp:SignedSupportingTokens</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826567" name="_Toc161826567"></a><a
      id="_EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens_"
      name="_EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens_"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.6</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>EncryptedSupportingTokens
      Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="MsoNormal">Encrypted supporting tokens are supporting tokens (See section
      8.1) that are included in the security header and MUST be encrypted when they
      appear in the security header. Element encryption SHOULD be used for encrypting
      these tokens. The encrypted supporting tokens can be added to any SOAP message and
      do not require the &ldquo;message signature&rdquo; being present before the
      encrypted supporting tokens are added.</p>
      <p class="MsoNormal">The syntax for the sp:EncryptedSupportingTokens differs from
      the syntax of sp:SupportingTokens only in the name of the assertion itself. All
      nested policy is as per the sp:SupportingTokens assertion.</p>
      <p class="MsoNormal">The encrypted supporting tokens SHOULD be used only when the
      sender cannot provide the &ldquo;message signature&rdquo; and it is RECOMMENDED
      that the receiver employs some security mechanisms external to the message to
      prevent the spoofing attacks. In all other cases it is RECOMMENDED to use signed
      encrypted supporting tokens instead to ensure that the encrypted tokens are
      cryptographically bound to the message (See section 8.5).</p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826568" name="_Toc161826568"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.7</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens
      Assertion</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Endorsing, encrypted
      supporting tokens are Endorsing supporting tokens</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>(See section 8.3) that are also encrypted
      when they appear in the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>wsse:SecurityHeader. Element Encryption
      SHOULD be used for encrypting the supporting tokens.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The syntax for the
      sp:EndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens differs from</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>the syntax of sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens
      only in the</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>n</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>ame of the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertion itself. All nested policy is as
      per the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:EndorsingSupportingTokens
      assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826569" name="_Toc161826569"></a><a
      id="_SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingT"
      name="_SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingT"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.8</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens
      Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signed, endorsing,
      encrypted supporting tokens are signed, endorsing</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>supporting tokens (See section 8.4) that
      are also encrypted when they</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>appear in the wsse:SecurityHeader. Element
      Encryption SHOULD be used for encrypting the supporting tokens.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The syntax for the
      sp:SignedEndorsingEncryptedSupportingTokens differs</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>from the syntax of
      sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens only in the name</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>of the assertion itself. All nested policy
      is as per the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens
      assertion.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc104103974" name="_Toc104103974"></a><a id="_Toc161826570"
      name="_Toc161826570"><span style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>8.9</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Interaction between [Token
      Protection] property and supporting token assertions</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Token
      Protection] (see Section 6.5) is true, then each signature covers the token that
      generated that signature and the following statements hold with respect to the
      various tokens that sign or are signed;</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The message signature, generated
      from the [Initiator Token] in the Asymmetric Binding case or the [Signature Token]
      in the Symmetric binding case, covers that token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Endorsing signatures cover the main
      signature and the endorsing token.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>For signed, endorsing supporting
      tokens, the supporting token is signed twice, once by the message signature and
      once by the endorsing signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>In addition, signed
      supporting tokens are covered by the message signature, although this is
      independent of [Token Protection].</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826571" name="_Toc161826571"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.10</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example policy
      containing supporting token assertions:</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;!-- Example Endpoint Policy --&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SymmetricBinding xmlns:sp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IssuedToken
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;...&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:IssuedToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic256
        /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;sp:UsernameToken
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;sp:X509Token
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once" &gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10
        /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
      sp:SignedSupportingTokens assertion in the above policy indicates that a Username
      Token must be included in the security header and covered by the message signature.
      The sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens assertion indicates that an X509 certificate
      must be included in the security header and covered by the message signature. In
      addition, a signature over the message signature based on the key material
      associated with the X509 certificate must be included in the security
      header.</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc73939889" name="_Toc73939889"></a><a id="_Toc161826572"
        name="_Toc161826572"></a><a id="_Toc104103975" name="_Toc104103975"></a><a
        id="_Toc96244191" name="_Toc96244191"></a><a id="_Toc95877137"
        name="_Toc95877137"></a><a id="_Toc95113142" name="_Toc95113142"></a><a
        id="_Toc85362849" name="_Toc85362849"></a><a id="_Toc85350294"
        name="_Toc85350294"></a><a id="_12.4._Confidentiality_Requirements"
        name="_12.4._Confidentiality_Requirements"></a><a
        id="_12.6._License_Exchange/Issuance_Req"
        name="_12.6._License_Exchange/Issuance_Req"></a><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>9<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
        <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP Message Security</span>
        <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Options</span></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There are several
      optional aspects to the WSS: SOAP Message Security specification that are
      independent of the trust and token taxonomies. This section describes another class
      of properties and associated assertions that indicate the supported aspects of WSS:
      SOAP Message Security.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
      assertions defined here MUST apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The properties and
      assertions dealing with token references defined in this section indicate whether
      the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process a given reference mechanism, or
      whether the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such references are
      encountered.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: This approach
      is chosen because:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A)<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[WSS: SOAP Message Security] allows for
      multiple equivalent reference mechanisms to be used in a single
      reference.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>B)<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>In a multi-message exchange, a token may
      be referenced using different mechanisms depending on which of a series of messages
      is being secured.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a message sent to
      a recipient does not adhere to the recipient&rsquo;s policy the recipient MAY raise
      a</span> <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse:InvalidSecurity</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>fault.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP
      Message Security 1.</span></u></b><b><u><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>0 Properties</span></u></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Direct
      References]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
      indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process direct token
      references (by ID or URI reference). This property always has a value of 'true'.
      i.e. All implementations MUST be able to process such references.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Key Identifier
      References]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
      key-specific identifier token references. A value of 'true' indicates that the
      initiator and recipient MUST be able to generate and process such references. A
      value of 'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such
      references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such references
      are encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
      has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Issuer Serial
      References]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
      references using the issuer and token serial number. A value of 'true' indicates
      that the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value
      of 'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such
      references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such references
      are encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property
      has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[External URI
      References]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
      references to tokens outside the message using URIs. A value of 'true' indicates
      that the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value
      of 'false' indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such
      references and that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such references
      are encountered.</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Embedded Token
      References]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
      references that contain embedded tokens. A value of 'true' indicates that the
      initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value of 'false'
      indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such references and
      that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such references are
      encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property has
      a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><span
      style='text-decoration:none'>&nbsp;</span></span></u></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WSS: SOAP
      Message Security 1.1 Properties</span></u></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Thumbprint
      References]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
      references using token thumbprints. A value of 'true' indicates that the initiator
      and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value of 'false' indicates
      that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such references and that the
      initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such references are
      encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property has
      a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[EncryptedKey
      References]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether the initiator and recipient MUST be able to process
      references using EncryptedKey references. A value of 'true' indicates that the
      initiator and recipient MUST be able to process such references. A value of 'false'
      indicates that the initiator and recipient MUST NOT generate such references and
      that the initiator and recipient MAY send a fault if such references are
      encountered.</span><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This property has
      a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Signature
      Confirmation]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>elements should be used as defined in WSS:
      Soap Message Security 1.1. If the value is 'true',</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>elements MUST be used and signed by the
      message signature. If the value is 'false', signature confirmation elements MUST
      NOT be used. The value of this property applies to all signatures that are included
      in the security header. This property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826573" name="_Toc161826573"></a><a id="_Toc104103976"
      name="_Toc104103976"></a><a id="_Wss10_Assertion" name="_Wss10_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>9.1</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss10 Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Wss10 assertion
      allows you to specify which WSS: SOAP Message Security 1.0 options are
      supported.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:Wss10 xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:Wss10&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss10</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a WSS10 assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss10/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This indicates
      a policy that controls WSS: SOAP Message Security 1.0 options.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Key Identifier References]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Issuer Serial References]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [External URI References] property
      is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss10/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Embedded Token References]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826574" name="_Toc161826574"></a><a id="_Toc104103977"
      name="_Toc104103977"></a><a id="_Wss11_Assertion" name="_Wss11_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>9.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss11 Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Wss11 assertion
      allows you to specify which WSS: SOAP Message Security 1.1 options are
      supported.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:Wss11 xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefThumbprint&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportRefEncryptedKey&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation&nbsp; ... /&gt; ?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss11</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies an WSS11 assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Wss11/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This indicates
      a policy that controls WSS: SOAP Message Security 1.1 options.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefKeyIdentifier</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Key Identifier References]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefIssuerSerial</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Issuer Serial References]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefExternalURI</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [External URI References] property
      is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefEmbeddedToken</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Embedded Token References]
      property is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefThumbprint</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Thumbprint References] property
      is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportRefEncryptedKey</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [EncryptedKey References] property
      is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Wss11/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Signature Confirmation] property
      is set to 'true'.</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc85362858" name="_Toc85362858"></a><a id="_Toc85350303"
        name="_Toc85350303"></a><a id="_Toc104103978" name="_Toc104103978"></a><a
        id="_Toc96244205" name="_Toc96244205"></a><a id="_Toc95877144"
        name="_Toc95877144"></a><a id="_Toc95113151" name="_Toc95113151"></a><a
        id="_Toc92518251" name="_Toc92518251"><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>10</span> <span
        style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></a><a id="_Toc161826575"
        name="_Toc161826575"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-Trust
        Options</span></a></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section defines
      the various policy assertions related to exchanges based on WS-Trust, specifically
      with client and server challenges and entropy behaviors. These assertions relate to
      interactions with a Security Token Service and may augment the behaviors defined by
      the Binding Property Assertions defined in Section 6. The assertions defined here
      MUST apply to [Endpoint Policy Subject].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><a id="_Toc96244206" name="_Toc96244206"></a><a id="_Toc95877145"
      name="_Toc95877145"></a><a id="_Toc95113152" name="_Toc95113152"></a><a
      id="_Toc92518252" name="_Toc92518252"><b><u><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'><span
      style='text-decoration:none'>&nbsp;</span></span></u></b></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><u><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-Trust 1.3
      Properties</span></u></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Client
      Challenge]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether client challenges are supported. A value of 'true'
      indicates that</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a</span> <span
      class="CodeChar"><span style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element is supported inside of an
      RST sent by the client to the server.</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A value of 'false' indicates that a</span>
      <span class="CodeChar"><span
      style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>is not supported.</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There is no change in the number of
      messages exchanged by the client and service in satisfying the RST. This property
      has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Server
      Challenge]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether server challenges are supported. A value of 'true'
      indicates that</span> <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>a</span> <span
      class="CodeChar"><span style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element is supported inside of an
      RSTR sent by the server to the client.</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A value of 'false' indicates that a</span>
      <span class="CodeChar"><span
      style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>is not supported.</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A challenge issued by the server may
      increase the number of messages exchanged by the client and service in order to
      accommodate the</span> <span class="CodeChar"><span
      style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallengeResponse</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element sent by the client to the server
      in response to the</span> <span class="CodeChar"><span
      style='font-size: 9.0pt'>wst:SignChallenge</span></span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element. A final RSTR containing the
      issued token will follow subsequent to the server receiving the</span> <span
      class="CodeChar"><span
      style='font-size:9.0pt'>wst:SignChallengeResponse</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element. This property has a default value
      of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Client
      Entropy]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether client entropy is required to be used as key material
      for a requested proof token. A value of 'true' indicates that client entropy is
      required. A value of 'false' indicates that client entropy is not required. This
      property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Server
      Entropy]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether server entropy is required to be used as key material
      for a requested proof token. A value of 'true' indicates that server entropy is
      required. A value of 'false' indicates that server entropy is not required. This
      property has a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: If both the
      [Client Entropy] and [Server Entropy] properties are set to true, Client and server
      entropy are combined to produce a computed key using the Computed Key algorithm
      defined by the [Algorithm Suite] property.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Issued
      Tokens]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property indicates whether the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wst:IssuedTokens</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>header is supported as described in
      WS-Trust. A value of 'true' indicates that the wst:IssuedTokens header is
      supported. A value of 'false' indicates that the</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Courier New"'>wst:IssuedTokens</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>header is not supported. This property has
      a default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[Collection]</span></b></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This boolean
      property specifies whether a wst:RequestSecurityTokenCollection element is present.
      A value of 'true' indicates that the wst:RequestSecurityTokenCollection element
      MUST be present and MUST be integrity protected either by transport or message
      level security. A value of 'false' indicates that the
      wst:RequestSecurityTokenCollection element MUST NOT be present. This property has a
      default value of 'false'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826576" name="_Toc161826576"></a><a id="_Toc104103979"
      name="_Toc104103979"></a><a id="_Trust10_Assertion"
      name="_Trust10_Assertion"></a><a id="_Trust130_Assertion"
      name="_Trust130_Assertion"></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>10.1</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Trust13 Assertion</span></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Trust13
      assertion allows you to specify which WS-Trust 1.3 options are
      supported.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><b><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Syntax</span></b></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;sp:Trust13 xmlns:sp="..." ... &gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportClientChallenge&nbsp; ... /&gt;?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportServerChallenge&nbsp; ... /&gt;?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireClientEntropy&nbsp; ... /&gt;?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireServerEntropy&nbsp; ... /&gt;?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:MustSupportIssuedTokens&nbsp; ... /&gt;?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireRequestSecurityTokenCollection /&gt;?</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireAppliesTo /&gt;?<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; ...<br />
        &lt;/sp:Trust13 ... &gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      describes the attributes and elements listed in the schema outlined
      above:</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Trust13</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This
      identifies a Trust13 assertion.</span></p>
      <p class="DefinedTerm"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>sp:</span><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Trust13/wsp:Policy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This indicates
      a policy that controls WS-Trust 1.3 options.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportClientChallenge</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Client Challenge] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportServerChallenge</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Server Challenge] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireClientEntropy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Client Entropy] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireServerEntropy</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Server Entropy] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:MustSupportIssuedTokens</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion indicates that the [Issued Tokens] property is set to
      'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust13/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireRequestSecurityTokenCollection</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the [Collection] property is set
      to 'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>/sp:Trust10/wsp:Policy/sp:RequireAppliesTo</span></p>
      <p class="Definition"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This optional
      element is a policy assertion that indicates that the STS requires the requestor to
      specify the scope for the issued token using wsp:AppliesTo in the RST.</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc104103980" name="_Toc104103980"></a><a id="_Toc96244213"
        name="_Toc96244213"></a><a id="_Toc95877152" name="_Toc95877152"></a><a
        id="_Toc95113159" name="_Toc95113159"></a><a id="_Toc161826577"
        name="_Toc161826577"><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>11</span><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Guidance on creating new assertions and
        assertion extensibility</span></a></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This non-normative
      appendix provides guidance for designers of new assertions intended for use with
      this specification.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826578" name="_Toc161826578"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>11.1</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>General Design Points</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Prefer Distinct Qnames</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Parameterize using nested policy
      where possible.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:Symbol'>&middot;<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Parameterize using attributes and/or
      child elements where necessary.</span></p>
      <h2><a id="_Toc161826579" name="_Toc161826579"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>11.2</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Detailed Design Guidance</span></a></h2>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Assertions in WS-SP
      are XML elements that are identified by their QName. Matching of assertions per
      WS-Policy is performed by matching element QNames. Matching does not take into
      account attributes that are present on the assertion element. Nor does it take into
      account child elements except for wsp:Policy elements. If a wsp:Policy element is
      present, then matching occurs against the assertions nested inside that wsp:Policy
      element recursively (see</span> <a
      href="http://www.w3.org/Submission/WS-Policy/#Policy_Assertion_Nesting"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Policy Assertion Nesting</span></a> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>[</span><a href="#wspolicy"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>WS-Policy</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>]).</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>When designing new
      assertions for use with WS-SP, the above matching behaviour needs to be taken into
      account. In general, multiple assertions with distinct QNames are preferably to a
      single assertion that uses attributes and/or content to distinguish different
      cases. For example, given two possible assertion designs;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Example">Design 1<br />
      <br />
      &nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;A1/&gt;<br />
      &nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;A2/&gt;<br />
      &nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;A3/&gt;<br />
      <br />
      Design 2.<br />
      <br />
      &nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;A Parameter='1' /&gt;<br />
      &nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;A Parameter='2' /&gt;<br />
      &nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;A Parameter='3' /&gt;<br />
      <br />
      </p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>then design 1. would
      generally be prefered because it allows the policy matching logic to provide more
      accurate matches between policies.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A good example of
      design 1 is the token assertions defined in Section 5. The section defines 10
      distinct token assertions, rather than a single sp:Token assertion with, for
      example, a TokenType attribute. These distinct token assertions make policy
      matching much more useful as less false positives are generated when performing
      policy matching.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>There are cases
      where using attributes or child elements as parameters in assertion design is
      reasonable. Examples include cases when implementations are expected to understand
      all the values for a given parameter and when encoding the parameter information
      into the assertion QName would result in an unmanageable number of assertions. A
      good example is the sp:IncludeToken attribute that appears on the various token
      assertions. Five possible values are currently specified for the sp:IncludeToken
      attribute and implementations are expected to understand the meaning of all 5
      values. If this information was encoded into the assertion QNames, each existing
      token assertion would require five variants, one for each Uri value which would
      result in 45 assertions just for the tokens defined in Section 5.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Nested policy is
      ideal for encoding parameters that can be usefully matched using policy matching.
      For example, the token version assertions defined in Section 5 use such an
      approach. The overall token type assertion is parameterized by the nested token
      version assertions. Policy matching can use these parameters to find matches
      between policies where the broad token type is support by both parties but they
      might not support the same specific versions.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note, when designing
      assertions for new token types such assertions SHOULD allow the sp:IncludeToken
      attribute and SHOULD allow nested policy.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <h1><a id="_Toc161826580" name="_Toc161826580"><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>12</span> <span
        style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Security Considerations</span></a></h1>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is strongly
      recommended that policies and assertions be signed to prevent
      tampering.&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is recommended
      that policies should not be accepted unless they are signed and have an associated
      security token to specify the signer has proper claims for the given policy.&nbsp;
      That is, a party shouldn't rely on a policy unless the policy is signed and
      presented with sufficient claims. It is further recommended that the entire policy
      exchange mechanism be protected to prevent man-in-the-middle downgrade
      attacks.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It should be noted
      that the mechanisms described in this document could be secured as part of a SOAP
      message using WSS: SOAP Message Security [<a href="#wss10">WSS10</a>, <a
      href="#wss11">WSS11</a>] or embedded within other objects using object-specific
      security mechanisms.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is recommended
      that policies not specify two (or more) SignedSupportingTokens or
      SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens of the same token type. Messages conforming to such
      policies are subject to modification which may be undetectable.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>It is recommended
      that policies specify the OnlySignEntireHeadersAndBody assertion along with the
      rest of the policy in order to combat certain XML substitution attacks.</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <p class="AppendixHeading1"><a id="AppendixA" name="AppendixA"></a><a
        id="_Toc161826581" name="_Toc161826581"></a><a id="_Toc104103983"
        name="_Toc104103983"></a><a id="_Toc100567689" name="_Toc100567689"><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A.<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;</span></span> <span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Assertions and
        WS-PolicyAttachment</span></a></p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This non-normative
      appendix classifies assertions according to their suggested scope in WSDL 1.1 per
      Section 4 of [WS-PolicyAttachment]. See Figure 1 in Section 4.1 of
      [WS-PolicyAttachment] for a graphical representation of the relationship between
      policy scope and WSDL. Unless otherwise noted above, any assertion that is listed
      under multiple [Policy Subjects] below MUST only apply to only one [Policy Subject]
      in a WSDL 1.1 hierarchy for calculating an Effective Policy.</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading2"><a id="_Toc161826582" name="_Toc161826582"></a><a

      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_RequiredElements_Assertion">RequiredElements
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 4.3.1)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_RequiredParts_Assertion">RequiredParts
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 4.3.2)</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading2"><a id="_Toc161826594" name="_Toc161826594"></a><a
      id="_Toc104103995" name="_Toc104103995"></a><a id="_Toc100567700"
      name="_Toc100567700"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A.4</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Assertions</span></a> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>With Undefined Policy Subject</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><a id="_Toc100567704" name="_Toc100567704"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The assertions listed in this section do
      not have a defined policy subject because they appear nested inside some other
      assertion which does have a defined policy subject. This list is derived from
      nested assertions in the specification that have independent sections. It is not a
      complete list of nested assertions. Many of the assertions previously listed in
      this appendix as well as the ones below have additional nested
      assertions.</span></a></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826595" name="_Toc161826595"></a><a
      id="_Toc104103996" name="_Toc104103996"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A.4.1</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>General Assertions</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><a href="#_AlgorithmSuite_Assertion"><span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>AlgorithmSuite Assertion</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 7.1)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><a href="#_Layout_Assertion"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Layout Assertion</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 7.2)</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826596" name="_Toc161826596"></a><a
      id="_Toc104103997" name="_Toc104103997"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A.4.2</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Token Usage Assertions</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>See the nested
      assertions under the</span> <a href="#_TransportBinding_Assertion"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>TransportBinding</span></a><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>,</span> <a
      href="#_SymmetricBinding_Assertion"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>SymmetricBinding</span></a> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>and</span> <a
      href="#_AsymmetricBinding_Assertion"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>AssymetricBinding</span></a> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>assertions.</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826597" name="_Toc161826597"></a><a
      id="_Toc104103998" name="_Toc104103998"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A.4.3</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>Token Assertions</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_UsernameToken_Assertion">UsernameToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 5.3.1)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_IssuedToken_Assertion">IssuedToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (Section
      5.3.2)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_X509Token_Assertion">X509Token
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 5.3.3)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_KerberosToken_Assertion">KerberosToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 5.3.4)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_SpnegoContextToken_Assertion">SpnegoContextToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (Section
      5.3.5)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_SecurityContextToken_Assertion">SecurityContextToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (Section
      5.3.6)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_SecureConversationToken_Assertion">SecureConversationToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; (Section
      5.3.7)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_SamlToken_Assertion">SamlToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 5.3.8)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_RelToken_Assertion">RelToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 5.3.9)</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><a
      href="#_HttpsToken_Assertion">HttpsToken
      Assertion</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
      (Section 5.3.10)</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <p class="AppendixHeading1"><a id="AppendixB" name="AppendixB"></a><a
        id="_Toc161826598" name="_Toc161826598"></a><a id="_Toc104104002"
        name="_Toc104104002"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>B.<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;</span></span> <span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Issued Token Policy</span></a></p>
      </div>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The section
      provides further detail about behavior associated with the IssuedToken assertion in
      section 5.3.2.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The issued
      token security model involves a three-party setup. There&rsquo;s a target Server, a
      Client, and a trusted third party called a Security Token Service or STS. Policy
      flows from Server to Client, and from STS to Client. Policy may be embedded inside
      an Issued Token assertion, or acquired out-of-band. There may be an explicit trust
      relationship between the Server and the STS. There must be a trust relationship
      between the Client and the STS.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Issued
      Token policy assertion includes two parts: 1) client-specific parameters that must
      be understood and processed by the client and 2) STS specific parameters which are
      to be processed by the STS. The format of the Issued Token policy assertion is
      illustrated in the figure below.</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="150" height="131"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image012.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The
      client-specific parameters of the Issued Token policy assertion along with the
      remainder of the server policy are consumed by the client. The STS specific
      parameters of the Issued Token policy assertion are passed on to the STS by copying
      the parameters directly into the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wst:SecondaryParameters</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>of the</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>RST request sent by the Client to the STS
      as illustrated in the figure below.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="399" height="214"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image013.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Before the
      Client sends the RST to the STS, it will need to obtain the policy for the STS.
      This will help to formulate the RST request and will include any security-specific
      requirements of the STS.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Client may
      augment or replace the contents of the RST made to the STS based on the
      Client-specific parameters received from the Issued Token policy assertion
      contained in the Server policy, from policy it received for the STS, or any other
      local parameters.</span></p>
