      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The Issued
      Token Policy Assertion contains elements which must be understood by the Client.
      The assertion contains one element which contains a list of arbitrary elements
      which should be sent along to the STS by copying the elements as-is directly into
      the</span> <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wst:SecondaryParameters</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>of the RST</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>request sent by the Client to the STS
      following the protocol defined in WS-Trust.</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Elements inside
      the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element MUST conform to WS-Trust [<a
      href="#wstrust">WS-Trust</a>]. All items are optional, since the Server and STS may
      already have a pre-arranged relationship which specifies some or all of the
      conditions and constraints for issued tokens.</span></p>
      <div style='border:none;border-top:solid gray 1.0pt;padding:6.0pt 0in 0in 0in'>
        <p class="AppendixHeading1"><a id="_Toc161826599" name="_Toc161826599"></a><a
        id="_Toc104104003" name="_Toc104104003"></a><a id="_Toc100567706"
        name="_Toc100567706"></a><a id="AppendixC" name="AppendixC"></a><span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.<span
        style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;</span></span> <span
        style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Strict Security Header Layout
        Examples</span></p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      sections describe the security header layout for specific bindings when applying
      the &lsquo;Strict&rsquo; layout rules defined in Section 6.7.</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading2"><a id="_Toc161826600" name="_Toc161826600"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104004" name="_Toc104104004"></a><a id="_Toc100567707"
      name="_Toc100567707"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.1</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Transport Binding</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section
      describes how the &lsquo;Strict&rsquo; security header layout rules apply to the
      Transport Binding.</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826601" name="_Toc161826601"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104005" name="_Toc104104005"></a><a id="_Toc100567708"
      name="_Toc100567708"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.1.1</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Policy</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      example shows a policy indicating a Transport Binding, an Https Token as the
      Transport Token, an algorithm suite, a requirement to include tokens in the
      supporting signatures, a username token attached to the message, and finally an
      X509 token attached to the message and endorsing the message signature. No message
      protection requirements are described since the transport covers all message
      parts.</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..." xmlns:sp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:TransportBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:TransportToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:HttpsToken
        /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:TransportToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic256
        /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Layout&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Strict /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:Layout&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:TransportBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:UsernameToken
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:X509Token
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once"&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10
        /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:Wss11&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;<br />
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This policy is used
      as the basis for the examples shown in the subsequent section describing the
      security header layout for this binding.</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826602" name="_Toc161826602"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104006" name="_Toc104104006"></a><a id="_Toc100567709"
      name="_Toc100567709"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.1.2</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to Recipient
      Messages</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
      initiator to recipient have the following layout for the security
      header:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>element.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens contained in the [Signed
      Supporting Tokens] property.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens contained in the [Signed
      Endorsing Supporting Tokens] property each followed by the corresponding signature.
      Each signature MUST cover the</span> <span
      class="CodeEmbedded">wsu:Timestamp</span><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element from 1 above and SHOULD cover any
      other unique identifier for the message in order to prevent replays. If [Token
      Protection] is 'true', the signature MUST also cover the supporting token. If
      [Derived Keys] is 'true' and the supporting token is associated with a symmetric
      key, then a Derived Key Token, based on the supporting token, appears between the
      supporting token and the signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any signatures for tokens contained in the
      [Endorsing Supporting Tokens] property. Each signature MUST cover the</span> <span
      class="CodeEmbedded">wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element from 1 above and SHOULD cover at
      least some other unique identifier for the message in order to prevent replays. If
      [Token Protection] is 'true', the signature MUST also cover the supporting token.
      If [Derived Keys] is 'true' and the supporting token is associated with a symmetric
      key, then a Derived Key Token, based on the supporting token, appears before the
      signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      diagram illustrates the security header layout for the initiator to recipient
      message:</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="346" height="512"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image014.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The outer box shows
      that the entire message is protected (signed and encrypted) by the transport. The
      arrows on the left from the box labeled Sig<sub>2</sub> indicate the parts signed
      by the supporting token labeled ST<sub>2</sub>, namely the message timestamp
      labeled TS and the token used as the basis for the signature labeled
      ST<sub>2</sub>. The dotted arrow indicates the token that was used as the basis for
      the signature. In general, the ordering of the items in the security header follows
      the most optimal layout for a receiver to process its contents.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><i><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to
      recipient message</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;S:Envelope xmlns:S="..." xmlns:wsse="..." xmlns:wsu="..."
        xmlns:ds="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id="timestamp"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:UsernameToken wsu:Id='SomeSignedToken'
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:UsernameToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken
        wsu:Id="SomeSignedEndorsingToken" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:Signature&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#timestamp" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#SomeSignedEndorsingToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:Reference URI="#SomeSignedEndorsingToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Body&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br />
        &lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826603" name="_Toc161826603"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104007" name="_Toc104104007"></a><a id="_Toc100567710"
      name="_Toc100567710"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.1.3</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Recipient to Initiator
      Messages</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
      recipient to initiator have the following layout for the security
      header:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>element.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the [Signature Confirmation] property
      has a value of 'true', then a</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element for each signature in the
      corresponding message sent from initiator to recipient. If there are no signatures
      in the corresponding message from the initiator to the recipient, then a</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element with no Value
      attribute.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      diagram illustrates the security header layout for the recipient to initiator
      message:</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="347" height="434"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image015.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The outer box shows
      that the entire message is protected (signed and encrypted) by the transport.
      One</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element labeled SC<sub>1</sub>
      corresponding to the signature in the initial message illustrated previously is
      included. In general, the ordering of the items in the security header follows the
      most optimal layout for a receiver to process its contents.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><i><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Recipient to
      initiator message</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;S:Envelope xmlns:S="..." xmlns:wsse="..." xmlns:wsu="..."
        xmlns:wsse11="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id="timestamp"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;[datetime]&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse11:SignatureConfirmation Value="..."
        /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Body&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br />
        &lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="AppendixHeading2"><a id="_Toc161826604" name="_Toc161826604"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104008" name="_Toc104104008"></a><a id="_Toc100567711"
      name="_Toc100567711"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.2</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Symmetric Binding</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><a id="_Toc99269308" name="_Toc99269308"></a><a id="_Toc96244173"
      name="_Toc96244173"></a><a id="_Toc95877119" name="_Toc95877119"></a><a
      id="_Toc95113124" name="_Toc95113124"></a><a id="_Toc85362812"
      name="_Toc85362812"></a><a id="_Toc85350257" name="_Toc85350257"></a><a
      id="_Toc79810730" name="_Toc79810730"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section describes how the
      &lsquo;Strict&rsquo; security header layout rules apply to the Symmetric
      Binding.</span></a></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826605" name="_Toc161826605"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104009" name="_Toc104104009"></a><a id="_Toc100567712"
      name="_Toc100567712"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.2.1</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Policy</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      example shows a policy indicating a Symmetric Binding, a symmetric key based
      IssuedToken provided as the Protection <a id="_Toc85362815"
      name="_Toc85362815"></a><a id="_Toc85350260" name="_Toc85350260"></a><a
      id="_Toc79810733" name="_Toc79810733">Token, an algorithm suite, a requirement to
      encrypt the message parts before signing, a requirement to encrypt the message
      signature, a requirement to include tokens in the message signature and the
      supporting signatures, a username token attached to the message, and finally an
      X509 token attached to the message and endorsing the message signature. Minimum
      message protection requirements are described as well.</a></span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;!-- Example Endpoint Policy --&gt;<br />
        &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..." xmlns:sp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;sp:IssuedToken
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:Issuer&gt;...&lt;/sp:Issuer&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/sp:RequestSecurityTokenTemplate&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:IssuedToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:ProtectionToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic256
        /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Layout&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Strict /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:Layout&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptSignature /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectTokens /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:UsernameToken
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:X509Token
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once"&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10
        /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;sp:Wss11&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;<br />
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        <br />
        </p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <br />
        &lt;!-- Example Message Policy --&gt;<br />
        &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..." xmlns:sp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Header Name="Header1" Namespace="..." /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Header Name="Header2" Namespace="..." /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Header Name="Header2" Namespace="..." /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br />
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><a id="_Toc96244176" name="_Toc96244176"></a><a id="_Toc95877122"
      name="_Toc95877122"></a><a id="_Toc95113127" name="_Toc95113127"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This policy is used as the basis for the
      examples shown in the subsequent section describing the security header layout for
      this binding.</span></a></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826606" name="_Toc161826606"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104010" name="_Toc104104010"></a><a id="_Toc100567713"
      name="_Toc100567713"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.2.2</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to Recipient
      Messages</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
      initiator to recipient have the following layout for the security
      header:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>element if [Timestamp] is
      'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute on the [Encryption Token] is
      .../IncludeToken/Once or .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Encryption
      Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys] is 'true', then a
      Derived Key Token, based on the [Encryption Token]. This Derived Key Token is used
      for encryption.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A reference list including references to
      encrypted items. If [Signature Protection] is 'true', then the reference list MUST
      include a reference to the message signature. If [Protection Order] is
      'SignBeforeEncrypting', then the reference list MUST include a reference to all the
      message parts specified in the EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. If [Derived
      Keys] is 'true', then the key in the token from 3 above MUST be used, otherwise the
      key in the [Encryption Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens from the [Signed Supporting
      Tokens] and [Signed Endorsing Supporting Tokens] properties whose</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute is .../IncludeToken/Once or
      .../IncludeToken/Always.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the [Signature Token] is not the same
      as the [Encryption Token], and the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute on the [Signature Token] is
      .../IncludeToken/Once or .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Signature
      Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys] is 'true', then a
      Derived Key Token based on the [Signature Token]. This Derived Key Token is used
      for signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A signature over the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>from 1 above, any tokens from 5 above
      regardless of whether they are included in the message, and any message parts
      specified in SignedParts assertions in the policy. If [Token Protection] is 'true',
      the signature MUST cover the [Signature Token] regardless of whether it is included
      in the message. If [Derived Keys] is 'true', the key in the token from 7 above MUST
      be used, otherwise the key in the [Signature Token] from 6 above.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>9.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signatures covering the main signature
      from 8 above for any tokens from the [Endorsing Supporting Tokens] and [Signed
      Endorsing Supporting Tokens] properties. If [Token Protection] is 'true', the
      signature MUST also cover the endorsing token. If [Derived Keys] is 'true' and the
      endorsing token is associated with a symmetric key, then a Derived Key Token, based
      on the endorsing token, appears before the signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>10.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Protection Order] is
      'EncryptBeforeSigning', then a reference list referencing all the message parts
      specified in EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. If [Derived Keys] is 'true',
      then the key in the token from 3 above MUST be used, otherwise the key in the
      [Encryption Token] from 2 above.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      diagram illustrates the security header layout for the initiator to recipient
      message:</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="575" height="627"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image016.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The arrows on the
      right indicate parts that were signed as part of the message signature labeled
      Sig<sub>1</sub>. The dashed arrows on the left from the box labeled Sig<sub>2</sub>
      indicate the parts signed by the supporting token labeled ST<sub>2</sub>, namely
      the message signature labeled Sig<sub>1</sub> and the token used as the basis for
      the signature labeled ST<sub>2</sub>. The arrows on the left from boxes labeled
      Ref<sub>1</sub> indicate references to parts encrypted using a key based on the
      Shared Secret Token labeled ST<sub>1</sub>. The dotted arrows inside the box
      labeled Security indicate the token that was used as the basis for each
      cryptographic operation. In general, the ordering of the items in the security
      header follows the most optimal layout for a receiver to process its
      contents.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><i><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to
      recipient message using EncryptBeforeSigning:</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;S:Envelope xmlns:S="..." xmlns:x="..." xmlns:wsu="..."</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; xmlns:wsse11="..." xmlns:wsse="..." xmlns:saml="..."</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; xmlns:xenc="..." xmlns:ds="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;x:Header1 wsu:Id="Header1" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/x:Header1&gt;<br />
        <br />
        </p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse11:EncryptedHeader wsu:Id="enc_Header2"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext Header2<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;x:Header2 wsu:Id="Header2" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/x:Header2&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse11:EncryptedHeader&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id="Timestamp"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;saml:Assertion
        AssertionId="_SharedSecretToken" ...&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/saml:Assertion&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_Signature" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_SomeUsernameToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID="enc_SomeUsernameToken"
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext UsernameToken<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:UsernameToken
        wsu:Id="SomeUsernameToken" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:UsernameToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:Reference URI="#_SharedSecretToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken
        wsu:Id="SomeSupportingToken" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData
        ID="enc_Signature"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext Signature<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:Signature
        Id="Signature"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Timestamp" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#SomeUsernameToken" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#SomeSupportingToken" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#_SharedSecretToken" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Header1" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Header2" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Body" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:Reference URI="#_SharedSecretToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &nbsp;&lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:Reference URI="#_SharedSecretToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:Signature&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;ds:Reference URI="#Signature"
        &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#SomeSupportingToken" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:Reference URI="#SomeSupportingToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference URI="#enc_Body"
        /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_Header2" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Body wsu:Id="Body"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData Id="enc_Body"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:Reference
        URI="#_SharedSecretToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br />
        &lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826607" name="_Toc161826607"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104011" name="_Toc104104011"></a><a id="_Toc100567714"
      name="_Toc100567714"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.2.3</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Recipient to Initiator
      Messages</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages send from
      recipient to initiator have the following layout for the security
      header:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>element if [Timestamp] is
      'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute on the [Encryption Token] is
      .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Encryption Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys] is 'true', then a
      Derived Key Token, based on the [Encryption Token]. This Derived Key Token is used
      for encryption.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A reference list including references to
      encrypted items. If [Signature Protection] is 'true', then the reference list MUST
      include a reference to the message signature from 6 below, and the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>elements from 5 below if any. If
      [Protection Order] is 'SignBeforeEncrypting', then the reference list MUST include
      a reference to all the message parts specified in the EncryptedParts assertions in
      the policy. If [Derived Keys] is 'true', then the key in the token from 2 above
      MUST be used, otherwise the key in the [Encryption Token] from 2 above.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Signature Confirmation] is 'true' then
      a</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element for each signature in the
      corresponding message sent from initiator to recipient. If there are no signatures
      in the corresponding message from the initiator to the recipient, then a</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>element with no Value
      attribute.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If the [Signature Token] is not the same
      as the [Encryption Token], and the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute on the [Signature Token] is
      .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Signature Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Derived Keys] is 'true', then a
      Derived Key Token, based on the [Signature Token]. This Derived Key Token is used
      for signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A signature over the wsu:Timestamp from 1
      above, any</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>elements from 5 above, and all the message
      parts specified in SignedParts assertions in the policy. If [Token Protection] is
      'true', the signature MUST also cover the [Signature Token] regardless of whether
      it is included in the message. If [Derived Keys] is 'true', the key in the token
      from 6 above MUST be used, otherwise the key in the [Signature Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>9.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If [Protection Order] is
      'EncryptBeforeSigning' then a reference list referencing all the message parts
      specified in EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. If [Derived Keys] is 'true',
      then the key in the Derived Key Token from 3 above MUST be used, otherwise the key
      in the [Encryption Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      diagram illustrates the security header layout for the recipient to initiator
      message:</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="603" height="488"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image017.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The arrows on the
      right indicate parts that were signed as part of the message signature labeled
      Sig<sub>1</sub>. The arrows on the left from boxes labeled Ref<sub>1</sub> indicate
      references to parts encrypted using a key based on the [SharedSecret Token] (not
      shown in these diagrams as it is referenced as an external token). Two</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>elements labeled SC<sub>1</sub> and
      SC<sub>2</sub> corresponding to the two signatures in the initial message
      illustrated previously is included. In general, the ordering of the items in the
      security header follows the most optimal layout for a receiver to process its
      contents. The rules used to determine this ordering are described in Appendix
      C.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><i><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Example:</span></i></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Recipient to
      initiator message using EncryptBeforeSigning:</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;S:Envelope&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;x:Header1 wsu:Id="Header1" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/x:Header1&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse11:EncryptedHeader wsu:Id="enc_Header2"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext Header2<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;x:Header2 wsu:Id="Header2" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/x:Header2&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse11:EncryptedHeader&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id="Timestamp"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_Signature" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_SigConf1" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_SigConf2" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID="enc_SigConf1"
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext
        SignatureConfirmation<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse11:SignatureConfirmation
        wsu:Id="SigConf1" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse11:SignatureConfirmation&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData ID="enc_SigConf2"
        &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext
        SignatureConfirmation<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse11:SignatureConfirmation
        wsu:Id="SigConf2" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse11:SignatureConfirmation&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        <br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData
        Id="enc_Signature"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext Signature<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:Signature
        Id="Signature"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Timestamp" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#SigConf1" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#SigConf2" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Header1" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Header2" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:Reference URI="#Body" &gt;...&lt;/ds:Reference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/ds:References&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/ds:SignedInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;ds:SignatureValue&gt;...&lt;/ds:SignatureValue&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:Reference URI="#_SomeIssuedToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:Signature&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        style='color:blue'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span>
        &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:Reference URI="#_SomeIssuedToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference URI="#enc_Body"
        /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_Header2" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:ReferenceList&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'><span
        style='color:blue'>&nbsp;&nbsp;</span> &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Header&gt;&nbsp;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Body wsu:Id="Body"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedData Id="enc_Body"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:Reference
        URI="#_SomeIssuedToken" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;/wsse:SecurityTokenReference&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/ds:KeyInfo&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/xenc:EncryptedData&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/S:Body&gt;<br />
        &lt;/S:Envelope&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="AppendixHeading2"><a id="_Toc161826608" name="_Toc161826608"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104012" name="_Toc104104012"></a><a id="_Toc100567715"
      name="_Toc100567715"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.3</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Asymmetric Binding</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><a id="_Toc99269313" name="_Toc99269313"></a><a id="_Toc96244178"
      name="_Toc96244178"></a><a id="_Toc95877124" name="_Toc95877124"></a><a
      id="_Toc95113129" name="_Toc95113129"></a><a id="_Toc85362827"
      name="_Toc85362827"></a><a id="_Toc85350272" name="_Toc85350272"></a><a
      id="_Toc79810745" name="_Toc79810745"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This section describes how the
      &lsquo;Strict&rsquo; security header layout rules apply to the Asymmetric
      Binding.</span></a></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826609" name="_Toc161826609"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104013" name="_Toc104104013"></a><a id="_Toc100567716"
      name="_Toc100567716"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.3.1</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Policy</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      example shows a policy indicating an Asymmetric Binding, an X509 token as the
      [Initiator Token], an X509 token as the [Recipient Token]<a id="_Toc85362830"
      name="_Toc85362830"></a><a id="_Toc85350275" name="_Toc85350275"></a><a
      id="_Toc79810748" name="_Toc79810748">, an algorithm suite, a requirement to
      encrypt the message parts before signing, a requirement to encrypt the message
      signature, a requirement to include tokens in the message signature and the
      supporting signatures, a requirement to include</a></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsse11:SignatureConfirmation</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>elements, a username token attached to the
      message, and finally an X509 token attached to the message and endorsing the
      message signature. Minimum message protection requirements are described as
      well.</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;!-- Example Endpoint Policy --&gt;<br />
        &lt;wsp:Policy xmlns:wsp="..." xmlns:sp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:X509Token
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Always" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:RecipientToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:X509Token
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Always" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:InitiatorToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Basic256 /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:AlgorithmSuite&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Layout&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Strict /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:Layout&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:IncludeTimestamp /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&lt;sp:EncryptBeforeSigning /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptSignature /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:ProtectTokens /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:AsymmetricBinding&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:UsernameToken
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedEncryptedSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:X509Token
        sp:IncludeToken=".../IncludeToken/Once"&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:WssX509v3Token10
        /&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/sp:X509Token&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedEndorsingSupportingTokens&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:Wss11&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:RequireSignatureConfirmation /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:Wss11&gt;<br />
        &lt;/wsp:Policy&gt;<br />
        <br />
        </p>
      </div>
      <p class="MsoNormal"><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;!-- Example Message Policy --&gt;<br />
        &lt;wsp:All xmlns:wsp="..." xmlns:sp="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Header Name="Header1" Namespace="..." /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Header Name="Header2" Namespace="..." /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:SignedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Header Name="Header2" Namespace="..." /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;sp:Body/&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;/sp:EncryptedParts&gt;<br />
        &lt;/wsp:All&gt;</p>
      </div>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>This policy is used
      as the basis for the examples shown in the subsequent section describing the
      security header layout for this binding.</span></p>
      <p class="AppendixHeading3"><a id="_Toc161826610" name="_Toc161826610"></a><a
      id="_Toc104104014" name="_Toc104104014"></a><a id="_Toc100567717"
      name="_Toc100567717"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>C.3.2</span>
      <span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to Recipient
      Messages</span></a></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Messages sent from
      initiator to recipient have the following layout:</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>1.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>element if [Timestamp] is
      'true'.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>2.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a [Recipient Token] is specified, and
      the associated</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute is .../IncludeToken/Once or
      .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Recipient Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>3.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a [Recipient Token] is specified and
      [Protection Order] is 'SignBeforeEncrypting' or [SignatureProtection] is 'true'
      then an xenc:EncryptedKey element, containing a key encrypted for the recipient.
      The xenc:EncryptedKey element MUST include an xenc:ReferenceList containing a
      reference to all the message parts specified in EncryptedParts assertions in the
      policy. If [Signature Protection] is 'true' then the reference list MUST contain a
      reference to the message signature from 6 below. It is an error if [Signature
      Protection] is 'true' and there is not a message signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>4.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Any tokens from the supporting tokens
      properties (as defined in section 8) whose</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute is .../IncludeToken/Once or
      .../IncludeToken/Always.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>5.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If an [Initiator Token] is specified, and
      the associated</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>sp:IncludeToken</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>attribute is .../IncludeToken/Once or
      .../IncludeToken/Always, then the [Initiator Token].</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>6.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>A signature based on the key in the
      [Initiator Token] if specified, over the</span> <span
      style='font-family:"Courier New"'>wsu:Timestamp</span> <span
      style='font-family: "Arial","sans-serif"'>from 1 above, any tokens from 4 above
      regardless of whether they are included in the message, and any message parts
      specified in SignedParts assertions in the policy. If [Token Protection] is 'true',
      the signature MUST also cover the [Initiator Token] regardless of whether it is
      included in the message.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>7.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Signatures for tokens from the [Endorsing
      Supporting Tokens] and [Signed Endorsing Supporting Tokens] properties. If [Derived
      Keys] is 'true' and the supporting token is associated with a symmetric key, then a
      Derived Key Token, based on the supporting token, appears before the signature. If
      [Token Protection] is 'true', the signature MUST also cover the supporting token
      regardless of whether it is included in the message.</span></p>
      <p class="Text" style='margin-left:.5in;text-indent:-.25in'><span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>8.<span
      style='font:7.0pt "Times New Roman"'>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span> <span
      style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>If a [Recipient Token] is specified and
      [Protection Order] is 'EncryptBeforeSigning' then if [Signature Protection] is
      'false' then an xenc:EncryptedKey element, containing a key encrypted for the
      recipient and a reference list, else if [Signature Protection] is 'true', a
      reference list. The reference list includes a reference to all the message parts
      specified in EncryptedParts assertions in the policy. The encrypted parts MUST
      reference the key contained in the xenc:EncryptedKey element from 3
      above.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The following
      diagram illustrates the security header layout for the initiator to recipient
      messages:</span></p>
      <p class="Figure"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'><img border="0"
      width="591" height="570"
      src="ws-securitypolicy-1.2-spec-os_files/image018.gif" /></span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>The arrows on the
      right indicate parts that were signed as part of the message signature labeled
      Sig<sub>2</sub> using the [Initiator Token] labeled ST<sub>2</sub>. The dashed
      arrows on the left from the box labeled Sig<sub>3</sub> indicate the parts signed
      by the supporting token ST<sub>3</sub>, namely the message signature
      Sig<sub>2</sub> and the token used as the basis for the signature labeled
      ST<sub>3</sub>. The arrows on the left from boxes labeled EK<sub>1</sub> indicate
      references to parts encrypted using a key encrypted for the [Recipient Token]
      labeled ST<sub>1</sub>. The arrows on the left from boxes labeled Ref<sub>1</sub>
      indicate additional references to parts encrypted using the key contained in the
      encrypted key labeled EK<sub>1</sub>. The dotted arrows inside the box labeled
      Security indicate the token used as the basis for each cryptographic operation. In
      general, the ordering of the items in the security header follows the most optimal
      layout for a receiver to process its contents. The rules used to determine this
      ordering are described in Appendix C.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>&nbsp;</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Note: In most
      typical scenarios, the recipient key is not included in the message, but rather the
      encrypted key contains an external reference to the token containing the encryption
      key. The diagram illustrates how one might attach a security token related to the
      encrypted key for completeness. One possible use-case for this approach might be a
      stack which does not support the STR Dereferencing Transform, but wishes to include
      the encryption token in the message signature.</span></p>
      <p class="Text"><span style='font-family:"Arial","sans-serif"'>Initiator to
      recipient message <i>Example</i></span></p>
      <div
      style='border-top:solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-left:none;border-bottom: solid windowtext 1.0pt;border-right:none;padding:3.0pt 0in 3.0pt 0in; background:#D9D9D9;margin-left:.3in;margin-right:.3in'>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &lt;S:Envelope&nbsp; xmlns:S="..." xmlns:x="..." xmlns:wsu="..."</p>
        <p class="Code" style='margin:0in;margin-bottom:.0001pt;background:#D9D9D9'>
        &nbsp;&nbsp; xmlns:wsse11="..." xmlns:wsse="..." xmlns:xenc="..."
        xmlns:ds="..."&gt;<br />
        &nbsp; &lt;S:Header&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;x:Header1 wsu:Id="Header1" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/x:Header1&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse11:EncryptedHeader wsu:Id="enc_Header2"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;!-- Plaintext Header2<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;x:Header2 wsu:Id="Header2" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/x:Header2&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; --&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse11:EncryptedHeader&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:Security&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsu:Timestamp wsu:Id="Timestamp"&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Created&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Created&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;
        &lt;wsu:Expires&gt;...&lt;/wsu:Expires&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsu:Timestamp&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;wsse:BinarySecurityToken
        wsu:Id="RecipientToken" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;/wsse:BinarySecurityToken&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:EncryptedKey
        wsu:Id="RecipientEncryptedKey" &gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; ...<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:ReferenceList&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_Signature" /&gt;<br />
        &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; &lt;xenc:DataReference
        URI="#enc_SomeUsernameToken" /&gt;<br />
